# Codex review — codex-condense (recreation)

_Repeated Codex review type, defined in this job's own guards layer (phase-guard augmentation, 2026-11-14). Execution: Codex via the fleet feeding rule (one stream); the DEFINITION lives here._

## Standing header (every run opens with it)
1. **Consumer:** recreation's §9 Condense for the closing cycle + the decision priced: whether the cycle's lessons were faithfully encoded into the instruction layer (steps · mechanical guards · Jev definitions · Codex prompts), or whether an amendment claims a lesson its text does not carry.
2. **Working record:** the cycle's RUN.md (traces + returns) + INSTRUCTION.md as amended this Condense + guards/ as amended this Condense + verdict-history.md — the run builds from the cycle record and the amended files, and writes its return into the cycle record's Condense section.
3. **Body-write:** the return must end with a `BODY-WRITE:` line naming what it filed; a read-only run closes with "Body-write: none (read-only run)" and the consumer (the cycle record) persists the return verbatim.

## The review
Fires at §9 Condense close, after the cycle's amendments are drafted and before the cycle record closes. The run attacks:
- **Faithfulness of each amendment:** for every lesson promoted this cycle (dated, technique cited — §9 form), does the amended instruction/guard text actually encode the lesson such that a cold builder following only the text would behave differently? An amendment that names a cure in prose while leaving the operative step unchanged is classified AMENDMENT-THEATRE. Founding calibration shape: Amendment A5 — Owner Cures 1+2 landed as construction requirements AND a fifth lens in §5.4 AND flow-field guidance; a lesser encoding (a mention in a lessons list) would have failed this attack.
- **Four-layer coverage:** did this cycle's experience touch each layer — (1) instruction steps, (2) mechanical guard code/commands (guards.md), (3) Jev definitions (jev-prewave · jev-observe-exit · jev-plan-exit), (4) Codex prompts (codex-scoring-wave · codex-observe · codex-plan-review · this file) — and for each untouched layer, is the no-change a NO-DEFECT proof naming the evidence checked, or an escape? A reversed verdict, a defect that passed a gate, or a lens finding that recurred across two techniques (§9 pattern rule) MUST land on its owning layer or be named not-yet-amended with reason (the presence-evaluation rule).
- **Score-evidence binding:** every lesson citing a lens score cites the filed return (artifact codex/ path); a lesson priced from the seat's summary rather than the return is named.
- **Wave re-pricing honesty (§0):** if this cycle re-priced wave size or cadence, the return checks the re-price cites measured per-item cost from THIS cycle's filings — a re-price from expectation is attacked.
The run does not re-score recreations and does not edit files; its REVISE verdict returns the named amendment to Condense for re-encoding before the cycle closes.

## Feeding contract
- ONE stream: this instruction + the cycle's RUN.md + the amendment diffs drafted this Condense (before/after text) + the §9 candidate-lesson list — never pipe + stdin together (fleet rule).
- One run per cycle (not per item): the consumer is the job's next cycle, which inherits the amended layer only after this review's verdict is filed.

## Demonstration / firing state
- **DEFINED** (2026-11-14). **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** No run has fired under this definition; Cycles 001–002 Condensed without it (their amendments A1–A5 stand as filed; this review prices future Condenses, it does not retro-judge them). First demonstration: the next recreation wave's Condense.

A9.1: no item adopts a moving background plate; the field is a still black plate in all four (the specimen's field never moves). A5 flow-field model does NOT apply to any wave item (no particle stream exists in this packet) — priced not-applicable with reason, here in the gate-out record itself (A9.5). A1 §2 typography precedent applies in its DOM form: footer names are DOM text rendered from CONFIG strings (never baked images, never traced outlines) — the runtime-sampling construction's lawful analogue for DOM text, stated with its reasoning here (A9.5): the purity question the precedent answers (letterforms generated from config text at runtime, not copied) is satisfied by construction, since the glyphs are the browser's rendering of the CONFIG string. Owner Cure 1 (intensity separation): the subject ink (dots, lines, settled names) renders FOREGROUND flat white at full intensity; the only dimmed elements are moving names under their sheen gradient (a motion-state signal, never a reserve field) — no background particle/reserve field exists in this packet, priced not-applicable beyond the sheen. Owner Cure 2 (phase exclusivity): bursts are serial by construction — one burst owns the stage at a time in every item; holds are completely static.

## §2c — Plan-gate REVISE cures (binding — builders receive §2 AS AMENDED by this section)
**ALL items — completed CONFIG contract (A9.6):** every item's CONFIG additionally carries, with these defaults: `footer.clip` {x 190, y 831, w 700, h 74} (the name clip box: ruleY 905 − lineH 64 − 10 = y 831, height lineH + 10) · `footer.ringStroke` 10 · `name.rollTravelPx` 64 (= lineH; a rolling name travels exactly one line height) · `sheen.stops` [{at 0, ink}, {at 0.5, #9a9a9a}, {at 1, ink}] (stop positions bound, not just colours). "Flat white" everywhere in §2 means `palette.ink` — a swap of ink recolours the settled state and the sheen's end stops together. Every item's phase machine is a pure function of CONFIG with half-open intervals; probes assert the boundary states printed per item.
- **T167:** `holdAfterMs` 500 is a CONFIG key in the table; `cycleMs` is DERIVED = growth.startMs + growth.durMs + holdAfterMs = 360+760+500 = 1620 (the swap growth.durMs→1100 derives cycleMs 1960 — the §2 wording "1600+..." is replaced by this derivation). `sheen` keys apply to the name during its roll window only. Single-name invariant: exactly one name exists in this build at any time (no outgoing/incoming pair — that is T170's device, not imported here). Boundary probe (plan-review charge): stateAt(1119).hubR < 32 AND stateAt(1120).hubR == 32 AND stateAt(1619).hubR == 32, filed raw.
- **T168 — source-time/allocation-time mapping (the UNBOUND-SCHEDULE cure; departures exposed, not hidden):** segment start = source 0.94 s. Source starts (segment-relative): B02 500 · B03 1220 · B04 1820 · B05 2260 · B06 2700, with §4 durations 480/400/280/280/240 and source holds 240/200/160/160. Allocation starts: 400/1200/1840/2320/2760 (departures: −100/−20/+20/+60/+60), durations PRESERVED exactly (480/400/280/280/240), allocation holds derived (= next start − start − dur): 320/240/200/160, end hold 1200 (3000→4200). The allocation's only systematic departure is a 100 ms shorter lead-in (400 vs 500) — printed here and on the comparison face. Census instants map by phase, with the offset printed: source 1.60 s = segment-relative 660 ms (S1 at p=(660−500)/480=0.333 of its draw, line partial, no dot) ↔ allocation S1 p=0.333 at t=400+160=560 ms; the §2 mapping's "p=0.55 at 664" line is CORRECTED: p=0.55 is an allocation-internal probe instant (t=664), not the census instant — both stand, labeled as what they are. # recreation — INSTRUCTION

`hadosh-job-file:v1` · born 2026-11-13 · the only carrier of this job's instruction layer; Condense amendments land here, dated, with the technique that proved them.

## §0 — Cycle shape (WAVE form — throughput correction, HQ 2026-11-14)
- Trigger: cron `study-recreation` or HQ charge. **A cycle is a WAVE, not an item:** up to **5 owed items** per cycle (starting price), taken in recreation-queue order, each carried to a scored verdict.
- **Fan-out:** each item's build + Codex wave is an independent agent task — the cycle spawns one subagent per item with the item's full §1–§5 charge; the seat coordinates, verifies each item's filings firsthand (scores.md arithmetic, purity of the filed build), and closes the cycle record. T15's attempt is the measured unit: one bounded build + four Codex runs, not a 6-hour job.
- Run directory `runs/<date>-cycle-<NNN>-wave-<K>/` opens BEFORE any building; its RUN.md holds the wave manifest (items, per-item parameter tables + verification plans summarized, links to per-item records) — each item ALSO gets its working record inside its artifact directory (`build-record.md`). Building first and writing the spec after is the inversion and does not ship.
- Artifact directory per item: `hidden_files/study/recreations/<technique-id>-<slug>/` — created at Plan, holds segment cut + recreation.html + comparison.html + scores.md + codex/ returns.
- **Batch re-pricing:** the first waves measure real per-item cost (wall time, failure modes); the wave size is re-priced from that evidence at Condense and reported to HQ — 5 is a starting price, not a law.
- **Cadence derivation (printed, not just scheduled):** while OWED + IN-PROGRESS items outnumber the RECREATED pool, waves fire every **3 h** — at wave 5 that is up to 40 first attempts/day against a 22-item backlog (≈0.6 days nominal to clear, before digestion adds more). As the catalogue approaches mostly-RECREATED, cadence relaxes toward maintenance — the relaxation is a printed judgment with its reason, filed as an amendment + reported, never a silent schedule drift.
- **Empty-pool form (promoted 2026-11-15, Cycle 010 Condense — candidate filed in Cycle 009's Condense; promotion condition was a second empty firing demonstrating the same shape, and Wave 8 is that firing; clause wording derives from the Cycle-009 candidate):** a wave with zero owed first-attempt items is a lawful cycle, not a skipped one. Its product is the finding form: (1) a pool census priced from the QUEUE recreation section, the catalogue register (TECHNIQUES.md + INDEX.md), the intake directory, and a binding artifact census (per-directory scores.md + codex/ return presence), plus board hand-offs where intake is pending; (2) the cycle-level guard firings — codex-observe in its cycle-level form, jev-observe-exit, jev-plan-exit, codex-condense — fired unconditionally, with per-item/per-build calls (codex-plan-review · jev-prewave · the five-lens scoring wave) printed NOT-DEMANDED-BY-FORM with their reasons; (3) the filings (HQ.json entry · verdict-history no-verdict line · lane heartbeat beat); (4) upstream intake state priced in the open (what is built, what stands unregistered/unadopted, which job owns the next act) — an empty pool here is never printed as an empty lane upstream, and the next wave's Observe re-censuses fresh rather than inheriting the finding. No item is invented, no practice re-attempt is misrouted, and no verdict is counterfeited for an unbuilt wave. First demonstration: Cycle 009 Wave 7 (2026-11-14). Second demonstration + this promotion: Cycle 010 Wave 8 (2026-11-15).
- **Completion-wave form (Amendment A8, 2026-11-16, Cycle 021 Condense — re-encoded under codex-condense REVISE before landing):** a wave whose predecessor stopped mid-Execute/Verify is completed by the next cycle: the completing cycle's §1 prices each unfinished item's on-disk state (artifact census + which prescribed calls already fired), and before any standing payload is fired its hash binding to the filed build is verified. Completion items occupy wave slots and count toward the §0 cap; new items fill only the remaining slots. The completing cycle restates per item the standing guidance dispositions and mechanics citations the item's Plan binds to (a prior cycle's §2b is evidence, not working memory), and carries each mid-attempt item to its verdict on the filed builds: hash-bound payloads and filed gate returns are consumed, never silently re-fired or rebuilt; where an item's record layer is missing (no build-record, no A6 legs, no gate filings), the completing cycle authors it from cited sources — the filed build plus the predecessor cycle's §§1b/2/2b — with the sources printed on the authored record's face and no fact claimed from builder memory, then fires the missing prescribed calls. First demonstration: Cycle 021 Wave 18 (2026-11-16) — T123 payloads consumed · T124 prewave consumed · T129 record authored from filed build + Cycle-020 §§1b/2/2b · T130 taken in the remaining slot.
- **Skeleton record at Plan close (adopted 2026-11-16, Cycle 021 Condense — practice Amendment A-C015-1's candidate form, charged to this job on its HQ board as rec-2026-11-16-skeleton-disposition; NON-SKIPPABLE):** before Execute opens, the wave writes (a) its HQ.json board entry skeleton — cycle id, the wave's items, state "planned — Execute not yet run" — and (b) its lane heartbeat skeleton beat in the same state. The cycle's close then UPDATES those skeleton entries in place (same ids) with the outcome; it never creates the record at close. A cycle whose board/heartbeat record was first created at its close prints deviation **D-SKELETON** in its RUN.md. (Evidence: recreation Cycle 017 + Cycle 020 and practice Cycles 013 + 015 — cycles that completed Observe + Plan, died before close, and left no board/heartbeat trace; Cycle 020's five built artifacts stood unreported until Cycle 021's census found them. Cycle 021 itself prints D-SKELETON: the charge stood on the board before this cycle opened and its Observe missed it — the adoption lands here, first demonstration is the next cycle.)

## §1 — Observe (what good Observe means for THIS job)
1. Read the technique's catalogue entry (packet digest.md section + TECHNIQUES.md entry): mechanics, timestamps, why-it-works, evidence frames. View the evidence frames firsthand — the recreation is built from the technique's mechanics, and a mechanic the builder has not seen is a guess.
2. Read the original segment in motion: the archived source at the catalogue timestamps (the cut is made in §3, but Observe watches the segment in the source first and notes the sub-beats: what forms when, holds how long, dissolves how).
3. Extract the technique's VARIABLES: every property that could change without changing the technique (text content, phrase count, timing/durations, palette, scale, direction, density). A variable not named in Observe becomes a hardcoded accident in Execute.
4. Name the consumer: which production surface will lift this machinery (container type + the lane that owns it).
Gate out: mechanics summary + sub-beat timings + variable list + consumer, in RUN.md.
- **RESOURCE LIST (read in this order, every wave):** (1) `../QUEUE.md` recreation-queue section — current positions, OWED status, ownership split (first attempts here; scored techniques route to practice) · (2) the item's packet section in its digest.md + TECHNIQUES.md entry (style-library) · (3) the item's prior scores.md + codex/ returns + `verdict-history.md` lines, including named family siblings (e.g. T16 reads T15's filings) · (4) the evidence frames + archived source segment in motion · (5) this file's Amendments A1/A3/A5 guidance for the item's family (flow-field model + Owner Cures 1–2 for particle fields; runtime text sampling for typography) · (6) `guards/guards.md` command list.
- **Codex call point (Observe):** `guards/codex-observe.md` fires per item at §1 step 1 (source-record audit) on every cycle. Its receipts persist in RUN.md's Observe section; a cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.
- **Observe-exit call:** `guards/jev-observe-exit.md` fires at this gate on every cycle and judges the gate-out block per item before §2 opens for that item. The phase advances on the verdict's recorded disposition (applied, or set aside with reason; applied gaps are carried into §2 as named charges). An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## §2 — Plan
1. **Parameter table:** config object design — every §1.3 variable becomes a named config key with the specimen's value as its default. The recreation reads ALL of its content/timing/palette from config; literals inside the animation logic are a Plan failure.
2. **Construction choice:** the HTML-native construction that fits (canvas particle system, CSS keyframes, SVG draw-on, DOM animation, generated imagery + overlays) — chosen for the technique's mechanics, with the rejected alternatives named in one line each.
3. **Bar pricing (standing, priced at founding 2026-11-13):** consolidation requires Codex wave average ≥85 AND no lens under 70. Priced judgment: the average alone would let a fatal lens (purity at 40) hide inside three good scores; the per-lens floor is what makes the bar a proof rather than a vibe, and 85 is high enough that a PASS means production can lift the machinery without re-deriving it. The bar stands as charged unless a scored cycle produces evidence against it — then the evidence goes to HQ, the bar does not move silently.
4. **Verification plan:** swap test design (which config values change — at minimum the words; plus one timing and one palette value), purity checks (§6.1 greps + asset inventory), comparison sync method, and the Codex wave composition (four lenses, §5).
Gate out: parameter table + construction choice + verification plan, in RUN.md.
- **Plan's standing product:** the Execute checklist (§3 steps as an itemized list) AND the Verify checklist (each deliverable property with its checking instrument — swap-test altered values, purity grep patterns, comparison check, jev-prewave call point, five-lens wave composition) AND the failure-point evaluation (per item, priced from this job's record: convergent freeze · intensity collapse · phrase overlap · fitting limits · render-proof limitation), all in RUN.md at this gate.
- **Plan-exit call:** `guards/jev-plan-exit.md` fires at this gate on every cycle and judges the Plan product per item (parameter table · checklists · failure points · plateau priced in Plan per Amendment A2 — prior/family average + the stuck-lens hypothesis stated before Execute, not discovered at close) before §3 opens. `guards/codex-plan-review.md` fires alongside it for every item. Both verdicts are advisory: the phase advances on their recorded dispositions (applied, or set aside with reason). An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## §3 — Execute
1. **Cut the original segment** from the archived source at the catalogue timestamps (±0.5 s handles) into the artifact directory as `segment.mp4`, re-encoded for browser playback. The segment is EVIDENCE: it appears in the comparison labeled ORIGINAL, and nowhere else. Bulk media stays local (sync pointer rule).
2. **Build `recreation.html`** — one self-contained HTML file (the fleet's form): config object at the top, construction below it, no external assets except generated imagery the build itself creates and files beside it. No captions/subtitles anywhere in the build (fleet law) — text that IS the technique (e.g. the words being formed) is the subject, not a caption.
3. **Build `comparison.html`** — the deliverable's face: original segment and live recreation side by side at matched size, ONE synchronized control set (play/pause/restart drives both; the recreation's clock slaved to the video's currentTime or to a shared start timestamp), each side labeled (ORIGINAL — <specimen> @ <timestamps> · RECREATION — <technique>, parameterized). On a phone-width screen the two stack vertically with the controls still reachable (fleet tappability law).
4. **Self-verify before Codex** (§5 legs 1–3); a build that fails its own swap test does not spend a Codex wave.
Gate out: segment + recreation + comparison on disk; self-verify recorded.
- Execute follows the Plan's Execute checklist and fires the Verify guards named in the Plan's Verify checklist — no step is improvised past the checklist; a step the checklist cannot cover returns the item to §2 (REVISE), it is not invented in Execute.

## §4 — (reserved; keep numbering stable for trace rows)

## §5 — Verify (the Codex scoring wave + own checks)
1. **Swap test (own, mechanical):** load the recreation with altered config (different words, one changed timing, one changed palette value) — by URL parameter or config edit in a scratch copy. The technique must still read correctly. Record the exact altered values + the result. A break = FAIL, return to Execute; Codex is not fired on a build that fails its own swap test.
2. **Construction purity (own, mechanical):** grep the recreation for the original's media (segment filename, source filename, frame-sequence patterns, <video>/<img> pointing at specimen media) — any hit is a refusal-class defect (§6.1). Asset inventory: every asset the recreation loads is listed and is either code or generated/created by this build.
3. **Comparison check (own):** both sides play from the shared control; durations matched to the segment; labels correct; phone-width stacking holds.
3b. **Pre-wave Jev gate:** `guards/jev-prewave.md` judges the build record after legs 1–3 and before the wave (leg 4): FIRE-WAVE spends the wave; HOLD returns the build to Execute with its classification and the wave moves to its next item. The deliverable-class judgment for this job IS the five-lens scoring wave (leg 4, defined in `guards/codex-scoring-wave.md`) — no separate jev-verify file exists, by design: the wave's per-lens verdicts + leg 5's mechanical consolidation are the deliverable gate, and a second Jev over the same deliverable would duplicate them.
4. **Codex scoring wave (external):** FIVE reviewers, distinct lenses — **motion fidelity · timing/tempo match · construction purity · reusability/parameterization · readability** (the fifth lens is permanent since 2026-11-14, Amendment A5: formed-word legibility against the particle field — word-vs-reserve intensity separation present and sufficient · the word readable at phone size during its hold · background particles never competing at word intensity). Each run: instruction file opens with the standing header (consumer + the decision the run prices; the working record it builds from and writes to; the return must name what it wrote into the body), carries the technique's catalogue mechanics + the recreation's full source, and returns ONE lens score (0–100) + priced findings. Feeding follows the fleet's Codex rule (instructions + artifact in ONE stream; never pipe + stdin field together). Returns are filed verbatim under the artifact's `codex/` directory.
5. **Consolidation verdict (mechanical):** average of the five lens scores ≥85 AND minimum lens ≥70 → PASS. Else FAIL. The arithmetic is shown in scores.md; no rounding up across the bar.
Gate out: scores.md complete (four lens scores + returns pointers + verdict + arithmetic); tier hand-off filed to style-library (its board) with the verdict; TECHNIQUES/INDEX tier moves are style-library's act.

## §6 — Guards (standing, every cycle)
1. **Construction purity (refusal-class):** the recreation is HTML/CSS/JS + generated/created assets only. Embedding the original video, a frame sequence of it, or a traced redraw of a frame as the "recreation" is a refusal-class defect — the cycle stops, the defect is recorded, the build is discarded. The original appears ONLY as the labeled comparison side.
2. **Parameterized, not traced:** content, timing, and palette come from config. The swap test is the enforcement; a recreation that only works with the specimen's exact words is a copy, whatever its code looks like.
3. **Scoring integrity:** four lenses, four separate runs, returns filed verbatim before the verdict is computed; a missing lens = no verdict (the wave is incomplete, printed as such). Scores from a prior attempt never carry into a new attempt's average.
4. **Tier honesty:** until scores.md shows PASS, the technique is IN PROGRESS (attempt filed) or DESCRIBED (no attempt) — the catalogue prints the tier the evidence supports, and this job's reports use the same words.
5. **No captions/subtitles** in recreation or comparison (fleet law); technique text is subject, not caption.
6. **Production-law boundary:** a RECREATED tier proves the technique is replicable in HTML. It does not by itself clear the technique for a shipping short — production's own laws (asset construction, placement, ear) govern adoption. The catalogue entry states this boundary where a technique's construction would collide with a production law (e.g. particle construction vs the production cutout law — the recreation proves the *effect*; production decides its lawful construction).

## §7 — Faithfulness trace (adopted 2026-11-13 from the template draft)
Every RUN.md closes with the draft's fields, as fields not prose: trace table (one row per §; missing = UNACCOUNTED), choice log (≥2 alternatives; SINGLE-PATH printed), deviation register (draft §0 enums; empty prints EMPTY-BY-CHECK + checks), evidence binding (path + size/sha/line/re-probe; UNVERIFIED at the claim), git corroboration (UNCORROBORATED-BY-LOCAL-GIT until JOB-14's sync extension lands lane files and is verified — lane board lane-2026-11-13-003), size discipline (§8).

## §8 — Size discipline
Norm (wave form): up to 5 items per cycle, one artifact directory each, fanned out in parallel. A technique whose build balloons (a whole scene instead of the technique) splits WITHIN its item: the item's Plan product is the split — core mechanic first, ornament as a later attempt on the same directory. An item that cannot reach a scored verdict inside its wave files its state honestly (IN PROGRESS with what stands) — a wave never holds its other items hostage to one stuck build.

## Amendment A1 (2026-11-13, from Cycle-002 — T15 attempt 1)
1. **Convergent-lens rule (§5 reading rule):** when two or more lenses independently name the same defect, that defect is priced as the next attempt's primary cure, ahead of single-lens findings. (T15 attempt 1: motion 68 + reuse 64 both named the surplus-particle freeze during FORM.)
2. **Typography construction precedent:** runtime sampling of config-rendered text is the lawful letterform construction (purity 100, T15 attempt 1) — future typography recreations start from it rather than re-deriving purity.

## Amendment A2 (2026-11-13, the funnel design — score-delta floor rule, HQ's priced addition)
**Re-attempt floor rule:** a re-attempt whose average does NOT beat the technique's prior attempt average is not filed as silent progress — its scores.md entry carries a WHY section naming the plateau (what was changed, what did not move, which lens is stuck and the hypothesized cause). Grinding the same score without a named plateau is the ceremony pattern in miniature and is a deviation (PLATEAU-UNNAMED) in the attempt's RUN. Score deltas are reported in the library's growth accounting (style-library GROWTH.md) per attempt, not only at consolidation.

## Amendment A3 (2026-11-13, owner's refinement to the recreation law — UNIVERSAL obligation)
1. **The queue orders; it never exempts.** If a catalogued lesson is a motion graphic that can be recreated as HTML, its recreation MUST be created. Triage survives only as ordering + pacing. Classification rule, applied per technique at registration and printed in the open: (a) is it a motion graphic (its lesson is motion/animation behavior, not a still, a system, or a story structure)? (b) can HTML/CSS/JS + generated assets carry that motion? Yes+yes = **OWED**. A no on either leg is priced with its reason in QUEUE.md and the tier register (T24 static end card: no motion content — documentation asset; T25 two-world system: a design system whose component techniques are individually owed — not itself one recreation).
2. **Tier accounting consequence:** a motion-graphic technique at DESCRIBED prints as **OWED + its queue position** (style-library Amendment A3 enforces the face).
3. **Division of labor with the practice job:** this job owns FIRST attempts (unattempted techniques, queue order). Re-attempts of already-scored recreations belong to the practice job (lowest-scored first). A technique mid-attempt here finishes its verdict here before practice can pick it up.

## Amendment A5 (2026-11-14, the owner's own read on T15 attempt 1 — two cures + the fifth lens)
The owner heard the four Codex returns verbatim, converged with the wave on the frozen-flow defect, and caught TWO defects no lens named. Both are standing law now:
1. **OWNER CURE 1 — foreground/background intensity separation (readability):** particles forming the word render FOREGROUND (contrast/opacity/intensity separated); reserve particles render BACKGROUND (dimmer). Same-intensity fields make formed words unreadable — in the original, the separation is part of why the words read at all. Any particle-field recreation carries this separation by construction.
2. **OWNER CURE 2 — strict phrase sequencing (phase exclusivity):** a phrase's formation may not begin until the previous phrase's dissolve has completed; one word's lifecycle owns the stage at a time. Overlapping lifecycles are a defect class in any sequenced recreation.
3. **THE FIFTH LENS — READABILITY (permanent, §5.4):** the founding evidence is priced: four lenses scored a recreation whose words fight their own background, and none owned legibility. The owner's expectation now has a judge (the golden rule). Attempt 2 onward is scored by five lenses; the bar form is unchanged (average ≥85, no lens <70) with the fifth lens inside both terms. Attempts scored under the four-lens wave keep their filed averages — cross-wave comparisons print the lens count (a 4-lens 78.5 and a 5-lens score are different instruments; deltas across the boundary are labeled as such).
4. **Flow-field construction model (HQ's implementation note, standing guidance for particle techniques):** model the pool as ONE constant flow field, not per-phase animations — every particle always carries the stream's directional velocity; forming = BORROWING particles out of the flow onto targets, where they keep low-amplitude jitter (never zero velocity), with a guaranteed reserve fraction that is never borrowed; dissolving = RELEASING them back to full stream velocity with continuity (no velocity replacement at phase boundaries). The intensity separation rides the borrow flag: borrowed = foreground, reserve = background. This single model carries Owner Cure 1, Owner Cure 2's clean stage handover, and attempt 1's convergent defect (frozen surplus) at once.

## §9 — Condense
Read the run's traces + Codex returns → candidate lessons (which constructions scored well on which lenses; recurring Codex findings across techniques) → label demonstrated/candidate/planned → promote into this file (dated, technique cited) + verdict-history.md → next cycle inherits. A lens finding that recurs across two techniques is a pattern: it becomes a §6 guard candidate with the two citations.
- **Condense Codex call point:** `guards/codex-condense.md` fires at Condense close — it attacks the cycle's drafted amendments for faithfulness (does the amended text encode the lesson) and four-layer coverage (steps · mechanical guards · Jev definitions · Codex prompts; each untouched layer carries a NO-DEFECT proof or a named not-yet-amended reason). A REVISE returns the named amendment for re-encoding before the cycle record closes. Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; a cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE. (Built 2026-11-14.)

## §10 — Delivery / reporting
- Completion reported on this job's HQ.json + lane heartbeat: technique, attempt #, five lens scores + average, verdict, artifact path.
- Owner-facing presentation routes through HQ. PASS or FAIL, the report carries the scores — a FAIL with its priced gap is a complete cycle; hiding it would be the defect.

## Judgment-layer presence evaluation (standing — owner's 100% order, 2026-11-14)
Before this job's Condense closes, every cycle, evaluate the judgment layer AS HOSTED IN THIS JOB'S OWN HOME (the `guards/` directory at the home root): (a) every judge this job's deliverables demand is present as its own file there — Jev calls (instruction + call command), deterministic guards, Codex review instructions for the review types this job repeats; (b) each is current against this cycle's experience (a verdict reversed, a defect that passed, a review that missed — each either amended its file in this Condense or is named in the cycle record as not-yet-amended with its reason); (c) any judge the deliverable demanded this cycle that does not exist yet is CREATED in this step or carried as a named build charge with pilot sequencing — never noted-and-dropped. The verdict prints in the cycle record: **JUDGMENT LAYER: COMPLETE** (all demanded judges hosted + current) or **INCOMPLETE** (missing/stale judges named). INCOMPLETE is a lawful print; a silent Condense is not. JOB-05's census reads this field fleet-wide.
Founding print (2026-11-14): JUDGMENT LAYER: **INCOMPLETE** — mechanical guards hosted + demonstrated (5/5); Codex scoring wave defined in-job and demonstrated in four-lens form (five-lens form demonstrates at its first wave); jev-prewave designed, **UNBUILT** — build charge carried in this job's Condense until armed.

**Ruling addendum (2026-11-14 ~20:55 EST — owner's judgment-call ruling):** arming = firing. This job's Jev/Codex steps are non-skippable and fire unconditionally in its next live cycle, raw responses filed in the run record; verdicts are advisory — applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense + the call-file amendment it teaches. The founding print above reads superseded where it phrases any call as gated, conditional, or UNBUILT-blocked; presence from the next cycle prices whether the prescribed firings HAPPENED (a cycle closing without one is INCOMPLETE).

## Amendment A6 (2026-11-15 — HQ owed-checks charge: the two legs are BUILT and WIRED)
The measured-fit leg and the config-census leg — candidacy text since Waves 5–6 — are built as standing guards: `guards/guard-measured-fit.md` and `guards/guard-config-census.md` (v3.1 codified, including application-site resolution for nested/array keys and the CSS-variable setter check). **Pre-wave step (non-skippable):** for every item in a wave, after the build and BEFORE the five-lens scoring wave fires, both legs run on the item's final recreation.html and their outputs file in the item's artifact directory (`fit-table.md`, `config-census.md`); the wave RUN cites each leg's path + hit count per item. A wave record lacking either leg's output for any item prints **INCOMPLETE**. Leg verdicts are advisory (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling): hits are cured pre-scoring where the builder can, or carried into the wave printed with their disposition; misses (a leg's false hit or missed defect) are recorded in Condense with the guard-file amendment they teach. Scope: the legs own the fit and config classes only — timing anchors, density judgment, and content failures are NOT theirs and are never presented as cured by them.

## Amendment A7 (2026-11-15, Cycle 013 Condense — pattern rule fired: T64 + T66 timing lenses)
**Schedule basis is a required Plan field.** The §9 pattern rule fired this cycle: two techniques' timing lenses independently priced the same defect class — a recreation whose schedule is silently re-based from the segment's measured timeline (T64 lens-timing −16: calendar entrances at 0.4/1.4 s vs measured ~4/5 s · T66 lens-timing-interim1 −18: opening state mismatching the measured window). Standing requirement, binding on every Plan from Wave 12 onward: each item's window-scope line in the wave RUN.md names its **schedule basis** — `segment-anchored` (the build's phases sit at their measured segment times), `t=0 allocation` (the technique's lifecycle is re-based to the build's own zero, with the re-basing named as an allocation), or `two-act` (a measured-window act plus a labeled allocation-model act) — and the item's build-record carries the same line. A Plan whose window-scope line lacks the schedule basis is incomplete in the same way a Plan lacking its swap values is (jev-plan-exit prices it). Landed by Cycle 013's Condense under the pattern rule; HQ may amend or retire it, and until then it is operative instruction, not candidacy.

## Amendment A9 (2026-11-16, Cycle 023 Condense — the five Cycle-022 NOT-YET-AMENDED landings, plus this cycle's pattern promotion)
Landed by Cycle 023's first Replan item, the named consumer of Cycle 022 §9's five outstanding amendments. Each clause is operative instruction, binding from Wave 21 onward.
1. **Plate construction is a Plan requirement (A9.1).** Any Plan adopting a moving background plate prices its construction in the parameter table: for techniques where a moving plate IS the technique (the RSVP family form), the plate is a seamless tiled pan — repeat period = pan wrap, pan clock continuous across cycle wrap, and **tiles endpoint-periodic** (a tile whose end state differs from its start state steps at the join — T139 motion lens, Cycle 023). For a case-only stream technique (T137's form), the Plan prices a still plate — any background motion exceeds the technique.
2. **Gate-order sequencing (A9.2).** No build script is written before the Observe-exit and Plan-exit returns are filed and consumed in the wave's §2b. A draft produced earlier is a deviation printed in §2b, never silently rebuilt over.
3. **Count assertion (A9.3).** Every table-driven build prints source-count vs built-count and halts on mismatch — in the item's build-record (builder side) AND in the wave record's own summary lines (seat side): a summary count in a gate or Verify section is machine-counted against the table it summarizes before that section closes, and a mismatch blocks the section's closure until the record carries the corrected count with its source note. (Founding catches: T134's truncated sentence table, Cycle 022 · the §2b 33-vs-37 summary in Cycle 023, caught builder-side before this landing.)
4. **Raw-output filing (A9.4).** Build-records file the raw harness output (swap test, purity grep, syntax check) from the first draft, not a summary of it; a build-record whose own-checks section carries claims without the raw output beneath them is an ASSERTED-CHECKS defect at jev-prewave.
5. **Gate-out reasoning inline (A9.5).** A gate-out section carries its dispositions' reasoning inline — the construction-precedent dispositions (A1 §2's scope, A5's applicability) are stated with their reasoning in the Observe gate-out itself, not only in a later Plan section the gate judge is not fed. (Founding miss: Cycle 023's jev-observe-exit GUIDANCE-DROPPED return — the reasoning stood in §2 while the judged record was §1.)
6. **Parameterization depth (A9.6 — pattern promotion, §9 rule: the reusability finding recurred across T139 + T140 in Cycle 023, both lens-minimum at 78).** Every subject-shaping value is CONFIG data, not a code literal: vehicle/geometry branch data, background-layer geometry and growth/opacity coefficients (a background layer's coefficients are subject-shaping when a lens can price them against the subject — the T140 dust classification is resolved this way), and plate tile data. The config-census leg (A6) prices literals of these classes as HITs from Wave 21 onward; guard-config-census.md's classification text is amended in the same landing's guard sweep or carries the not-yet-amended note in the cycle record that first applies it.

## Amendment A10 (2026-11-17, Cycle 026 Condense — the three bundled restatements whose named consumer was this Condense)
Landed by Cycle 026's Condense, discharging the carry recorded in Cycle 025 §9 item 1 and Cycle 026 §0b item 1. Each clause restates at instruction level a form already operative in the guards or gate contracts; each is binding from Wave 23 onward (Wave 23 complied with all three, filed in its run record).
1. **Prewave payload completeness (A10.1 — restates §5 leg 3b at instruction level; operative since Cycle 024 in jev-prewave.md's context contract).** The jev-prewave payload for every item MUST carry that item's A6 leg outputs (fit-table.md + config-census.md) verbatim. A payload lacking either leg's output is a feeder defect printed in the wave's §5; a prewave verdict priced without them does not discharge leg 3b.
2. **Plan census-anchor mapping (A10.2 — restates the Cycle-025 Plan form at instruction level).** Every schedule table in a wave's Plan prints its census-anchor mapping: the window-start offset (segment-anchored or t=0 allocation), the census states in segment-relative ms, and the prescribed state at each instant the table governs. A schedule that departs from its census prints its departure table beside it (the Wave-22 defect class: census printed in §1, schedule derived from other numbers, the gap found only by the timing lens at verdict cost). Plan-exit judges price an absent mapping or departure table as REVISE.
3. **Motion-census Observe form (A10.3 — restates the Cycle-025 Observe form at instruction level).** §1 files a frame census at ≤2 s spacing across each item's transitions (seat-extracted, viewed firsthand by the seat), full SHA-256 per frame, with the motion findings stated per item: which instants carry motion, which are holds, and what changes between census states. Findings priced from a digest's prose alone, without the census, do not close Observe.

# Codex review — codex-condense (recreation)

_Repeated Codex review type, defined in this job's own guards layer (phase-guard augmentation, 2026-11-14). Execution: Codex via the fleet feeding rule (one stream); the DEFINITION lives here._

## Standing header (every run opens with it)
1. **Consumer:** recreation's §9 Condense for the closing cycle + the decision priced: whether the cycle's lessons were faithfully encoded into the instruction layer (steps · mechanical guards · Jev definitions · Codex prompts), or whether an amendment claims a lesson its text does not carry.
2. **Working record:** the cycle's RUN.md (traces + returns) + INSTRUCTION.md as amended this Condense + guards/ as amended this Condense + verdict-history.md — the run builds from the cycle record and the amended files, and writes its return into the cycle record's Condense section.
3. **Body-write:** the return must end with a `BODY-WRITE:` line naming what it filed; a read-only run closes with "Body-write: none (read-only run)" and the consumer (the cycle record) persists the return verbatim.

## The review
Fires at §9 Condense close, after the cycle's amendments are drafted and before the cycle record closes. The run attacks:
- **Faithfulness of each amendment:** for every lesson promoted this cycle (dated, technique cited — §9 form), does the amended instruction/guard text actually encode the lesson such that a cold builder following only the text would behave differently? An amendment that names a cure in prose while leaving the operative step unchanged is classified AMENDMENT-THEATRE. Founding calibration shape: Amendment A5 — Owner Cures 1+2 landed as construction requirements AND a fifth lens in §5.4 AND flow-field guidance; a lesser encoding (a mention in a lessons list) would have failed this attack.
- **Four-layer coverage:** did this cycle's experience touch each layer — (1) instruction steps, (2) mechanical guard code/commands (guards.md), (3) Jev definitions (jev-prewave · jev-observe-exit · jev-plan-exit), (4) Codex prompts (codex-scoring-wave · codex-observe · codex-plan-review · this file) — and for each untouched layer, is the no-change a NO-DEFECT proof naming the evidence checked, or an escape? A reversed verdict, a defect that passed a gate, or a lens finding that recurred across two techniques (§9 pattern rule) MUST land on its owning layer or be named not-yet-amended with reason (the presence-evaluation rule).
- **Score-evidence binding:** every lesson citing a lens score cites the filed return (artifact codex/ path); a lesson priced from the seat's summary rather than the return is named.
- **Wave re-pricing honesty (§0):** if this cycle re-priced wave size or cadence, the return checks the re-price cites measured per-item cost from THIS cycle's filings — a re-price from expectation is attacked.
The run does not re-score recreations and does not edit files; its REVISE verdict returns the named amendment to Condense for re-encoding before the cycle closes.

## Feeding contract
- ONE stream: this instruction + the cycle's RUN.md + the amendment diffs drafted this Condense (before/after text) + the §9 candidate-lesson list — never pipe + stdin together (fleet rule).
- One run per cycle (not per item): the consumer is the job's next cycle, which inherits the amended layer only after this review's verdict is filed.

## Demonstration / firing state
- **DEFINED** (2026-11-14). **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** No run has fired under this definition; Cycles 001–002 Condensed without it (their amendments A1–A5 stand as filed; this review prices future Condenses, it does not retro-judge them). First demonstration: the next recreation wave's Condense.
# Codex review — codex-observe (recreation)

_Repeated Codex review type, defined in this job's own guards layer (phase-guard augmentation, 2026-11-14). Execution: Codex via the fleet feeding rule (one stream); the DEFINITION lives here._

## Standing header (every run opens with it)
1. **Consumer:** recreation's §1 Observe for the named wave item + the decision priced: whether the item's source record (packet section + prior attempt filings) is complete and internally consistent enough to Observe from, and what named gaps Observe must close firsthand.
2. **Working record:** the item's artifact directory (`hidden_files/study/recreations/<technique-id>-<slug>/`) + the wave's RUN.md — the run builds from the packet section and prior filings, and writes its return to the wave record.
3. **Body-write:** the return must end with a `BODY-WRITE:` line naming what it filed; a read-only run closes with "Body-write: none (read-only run)" and the consumer (the wave's Observe record) persists the return verbatim in RUN.md's Observe section.

## The review
An independent audit of the item's SOURCE RECORD before Observe prices it — the failure it exists against is Observe inheriting a packet gap or a stale prior-score claim as fact. The run checks:
- **Packet-section completeness:** the digest.md technique section + TECHNIQUES.md entry agree on mechanics, timestamps/window, and evidence frames; disagreements named with both citations. (Packet #1's founding gaps G1–G3 are the calibration shape: a packet can be adopted with gaps — Observe must know which gaps touch THIS technique.)
- **Prior-attempt record audit:** scores.md + codex/ returns + verdict-history.md for this technique and its named family siblings agree with each other (arithmetic recomputed, lens count printed — a four-lens 78.5 never silently read as a five-lens score); a scores.md claim with no filed return behind it is named as unbound.
- **Segment facts:** the archived source's window for this technique (timestamps, duration) is checkable against the packet's claim before §3 cuts it (guards.md command 5's pre-build leg).
The run does NOT judge the Observe working memory itself — that is jev-observe-exit's gate. This run audits the record Observe will read.

## Feeding contract
- ONE stream: this instruction + the technique's packet section + TECHNIQUES.md entry + prior scores.md/verdict-history lines for the technique and family siblings — never pipe + stdin together (fleet rule; a doubled stream voids the run).
- Fires per item at §1 step 1, before the mechanics summary is written — every item, including items with no prior attempt (the audit then prices the packet section's internal consistency as its finding).
- **Cycle-level form (amendment, 2026-11-14, Cycle 009 Condense — from that cycle's codex-observe set-aside):** in a wave with zero items the audit fires ONCE on the pool record (QUEUE section · TECHNIQUES register · INDEX state · verdict-history · artifact census). The packet fed to a cycle-level audit MUST carry the seat's binding census (per-directory codex/ return presence + the per-attempt binding basis) — an excerpts-only packet prices as an audit of excerpts, the feeder records the packet's limitation in the cycle record, and the auditor remains free to return NOT-CONFIRMED whenever the supplied evidence, census included, is insufficient (the requirement binds the feeder's packet, never the auditor's verdict). Every record line the census references without reproducing (no-verdict lines, status-lag rows, hand-off entries) is identified in the packet by subject + disposition — a referenced-but-unidentified line prices as a gap in the audit (amendment, 2026-11-15, Cycle 010: the fed census counted verdict-history's no-verdict line without identifying it; codex-observe's CONFIRMED-WITH-GAPS named the omission). First demonstration: Cycle 009 Wave 7 (return filed in that run directory; set-aside + miss recorded there).
- **Sequencing rule (amendment, 2026-11-15, Cycle 011 Condense — from deviation D1, priced as an escape by that cycle's codex-condense REVISE):** in a cycle-level firing, the seat files this call's return in the run directory BEFORE the jev-observe-exit judge for the same cycle is started, and the filed return is provided to that judge as part of its record. If the return is unavailable when the judge must start, the missing receipt is recorded explicitly in the run record (never silently absent). Founding incident: Cycle 011 Wave 9 — the seat filed the return while the judge was already running; the judge priced the absence as its Gap G4 and Plan had to cure it by receipt (RUN.md §2 P4, that cycle).

## Demonstration / firing state
- **DEFINED** (2026-11-14). **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** No run has fired under this definition; T15 attempt 1 (2026-11-13) predates it and ran no Observe-phase Codex audit. First demonstration: the next recreation wave's first item with a prior family record (expected: a particle-family or typography-family item citing T15).
- **Update (2026-11-14, Cycle 009):** under the owner's 20:55 ruling (arming = firing) the state language above is superseded. First firing landed at Cycle 009 Wave 7 in the cycle-level form (pool-record audit; return + set-aside in that run directory). The per-item form's first demonstration LANDED at Cycle 012 Wave 10 (2026-11-15): fired ×5 (T53/T54/T56/T57/T59, all CONFIRMED-WITH-GAPS, returns filed in each artifact's codex/codex-observe.md) — factual maintenance correction landed at that cycle's Condense on its codex-condense REVISE.
# Codex review — codex-plan-review (recreation)

_Repeated Codex review type, defined in this job's own guards layer (phase-guard augmentation, 2026-11-14). Execution: Codex via the fleet feeding rule (one stream); the DEFINITION lives here._

## Standing header (every run opens with it)
1. **Consumer:** recreation's §2 Plan for the named wave item + the decision priced: whether the Plan's construction choice will survive its own named failure points, or must be revised before Execute spends on it.
2. **Working record:** the wave's RUN.md (Plan gate-out block) + the item's build-record.md — the run builds from the Plan and the filed failure record, and writes its return into RUN.md's Plan section.
3. **Body-write:** the return must end with a `BODY-WRITE:` line naming what it filed; a read-only run closes with "Body-write: none (read-only run)" and the consumer (the wave's Plan record) persists the return verbatim.

## The review
An attack on ONE item's Plan, priced against this job's recorded failure modes — not a generic design review. The run attacks, in order:
- **The convergent defect:** T15 attempt 1's motion (68) and reusability (64) lenses independently named the surplus-particle freeze — matched particle count, no reserve, formed word static. Does this Plan's construction make that defect impossible by construction (flow-field model, never-borrowed reserve fraction, velocity continuity), or does it merely promise to avoid it?
- **The owner's two catches:** (1) intensity separation — is foreground/background separation a construction property (rides the borrow flag / layer), or a styling hope? (2) phrase exclusivity — is strict sequencing enforced by the phase machine, or by timing arithmetic that two phrases can overlap under?
- **Parameterization reality:** does the parameter table bind every §1.3 variable, and does the construction choice keep literals out of the animation logic (§2.1)? Where would a hardcoded accident hide in this construction?
- **The bar's arithmetic:** with the five-lens bar (average ≥85, no lens <70), which lens is this Plan's predicted minimum, and is that prediction honest against the family's filed scores? A Plan predicting no weak lens on a family whose filed minimum is 64 is attacked as unpriced.
- **Size discipline (§8):** is the item scoped to the technique's core mechanic, with ornament split as a later attempt — or is it a whole scene wearing a technique's name?
This attack fires for every item, including techniques with no family failure record (the attack then prices the plan against the fleet's recorded failure modes and says so).

## Feeding contract
- ONE stream: this instruction + the item's Plan gate-out block + Observe gate-out block + the family's filed scores.md cure list + INSTRUCTION Amendments A1/A2/A5 — never pipe + stdin together (fleet rule).
- Fires at §2 gate out, alongside jev-plan-exit; the two verdicts are independent (this run attacks construction risk, the Jev judges checklist/plateau completeness). A REVISE from either is advisory input, consumed before Execute: the seat applies it, or proceeds over it only with the disposition and its reason recorded in the wave's RUN.md Plan section (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling: verdicts are advisory, never a self-executing block).

## Demonstration / firing state
- **DEFINED** (2026-11-14). **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** No run has fired under this definition. First demonstration: the next wave item in the particle or typography family (T15's machinery siblings head the queue's priced reasons — T16 at queue #11 shares the pool; T03 at #2 heads the unattempted queue).
# Codex review — scoring wave (recreation)

_Repeated Codex review type, defined in this job's own guards layer (owner's 100% order, 2026-11-14). Execution: Codex via the fleet feeding rule; the DEFINITION lives here._

## The review
Five independent lens runs score ONE recreation attempt against the BAR: **average of the five lens scores ≥ 85 AND no lens < 70** (the recreation law; a pass at this bar is the only route to RECREATED).
Lenses: **motion fidelity** (does the recreation's movement equal the technique's described movement?) · **timing/tempo** (phase timings vs the original's measured window) · **construction purity** (parameterized construction vs tracing/embedding — a refusal-class judgment) · **reusability** (could production drive it from CONFIG alone for a different piece?) · **readability** (owner's fifth lens, 2026-11-14: formed words legible against their own particle field — intensity separation, phone-size legibility during hold, background never at word intensity).

## Feeding contract
- ONE stream per lens run: lens instruction file + the artifacts it judges, concatenated — never pipe + stdin together (fleet rule; a doubled stream voids the run).
- **Score-blind allowed packet (ADOPTED 2026-11-14, Cycle 009 Condense — the Wave-6 adoption decision executed):** lens payloads are the ALLOWED PACKET only (lens instruction · neutral technique facts · the attempt's recreation.html · a score-blind build record). scores.md is NEVER fed to a lens; no lens sees a prior score or another lens's return.
- **Identical-hash binding (ADOPTED 2026-11-14, Cycle 009):** all five returns of an attempt must bind to the identical final recreation.html SHA-256. When a lens catches an interim-build defect, the build is cured, self-verify re-runs, and the wave re-fires on the final build; the interim return is preserved verbatim (e.g. `codex/lens-motion-interim1.md`) and never enters the final average. Founding instance: Wave 6, T38 (interim motion 35 → cured → five lenses re-fired on the final build).
- **Every-firing preservation duty (ADOPTED 2026-11-14, Cycle 009 — practice A-C003-1 generalized):** every firing's payload + return is filed create-once in the attempt directory BEFORE any subsequent source change, payload regeneration, or re-fire, for every attempt number; a discarded interim return files as a printed deviation with the material recorded UNAVAILABLE.
- Every lens instruction opens with the standing header: (1) consumer = recreation job's §5 step + the decision priced (tier movement / cure list), (2) the working record it builds from and writes to (the artifact's codex/ directory + scores.md), (3) the return must end with `SCORE: <integer>` and a `BODY-WRITE:` line naming what it filed.
- Lenses run independently; no lens sees another's return.
- Returns file VERBATIM in the artifact's `codex/lens-<name>.md`; scores.md cites them; the seat recomputes average + min mechanically (guards.md command 4) — a wave average is never taken from a return's own arithmetic.

## Consumer
- Consumer: recreation's §5 Verify (tier recommendation) + the cycle's Condense (cure list, convergent-lens rule: a defect two lenses name becomes the next attempt's primary cure) + practice (the filed returns are its Observe input).
- Decision priced: whether this attempt is RECREATED-tier, and exactly what the next attempt must cure.

## Demonstration state
- Four-lens form: demonstrated 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (68/82/100/64 → 78.5, FAIL, returns on file).
- Five-lens form (readability born 2026-11-14): demonstrated Waves 1–6 (2026-11-14, 25 attempts) under the score-blind + hash-binding practice adopted at Wave 6.

## Adoption record (2026-11-14, Cycle 009 Condense — replaces the divergence notices printed here 2026-11-14)
Recreation's Wave-6 Condense adopted the practice-copy rules (practice Cycle-002 A-C002-4 + payload/binding corrections, and Cycle-003 A-C003-1's every-firing preservation duty) after applying and proving them in Wave 6, and charged the text amendment to the next Condense. That amendment lands here at Cycle 009: the three rules now stand in the Feeding contract above as this job's own text. The divergence between this file and practice's `codex-rescore-wave.md` is CLOSED on these three rules; the historical notices are retired by this record, not silently deleted (Wave-6 RUN.md Condense + this adoption record carry the history).

## Amendment — census-anchor mapping is a required timing-payload element (2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 025 Condense)
From Wave 23 onward, the **timing lens payload** for every item MUST include the item's **census-anchor mapping table** (window-start offset · each census state's segment-relative ms · the build's prescribed state at that instant, per the jev-plan-exit consistency leg landed the same Condense). A timing payload without the mapping is a feeder defect, printed as such in the item's build record; the lens prices the schedule against the mapping, not against the builder's summary of it. Forcing evidence: T160 Wave 22 timing 68 + T162 Wave 22 timing 64 (filed returns: the items' `codex/lens-timing.md`) — in both, the schedule the lens was asked to judge contradicted the census anchors the Plan claimed to bind, and the mapping that would have exposed the contradiction at Plan time existed nowhere as a single table.
# guard-config-census — the config-census leg, v3.1 codified (recreation + practice)

_Built 2026-11-15 on HQ's charge (owed checks, 2026-11-15 ~08:02 EST), codifying candidate v3.1 from recreation Cycle-008 Wave-6's Condense (which extended Wave-5's v3, which extended v2). Before this build the census existed only as candidacy text inside RUN records and as builder-run greps whose blind spots the scoring lenses kept finding. This file is the leg's standing definition. It owns ONE defect class: reusability failures whose mechanism is **config incompleteness** — shaping decisions living outside CONFIG, or CONFIG keys that promise control they do not exert (reusability stuck across Waves 3–6 and binding in practice waves since). It does NOT own timing, fit (guard-measured-fit's class), or content failures._

## What the leg checks (per item, on the final recreation.html)
1. **All-literal scan.** Every literal outside the CONFIG block is counted and classified: hex/rgba colors · durations · geometry values · numeric constants and formula coefficients. Each constant is classified in print as **subject-shaping** (it shapes what the subject looks like or how it moves → must be a CONFIG key; standing outside = HIT) or **structural** (loop bounds, indices, mathematical identities → exempt, classification printed per constant; the sr/3, sr/6 misclassification of Wave 6 is the cautionary instance — ratios that scale the subject are subject-shaping). **Addendum landed 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 024 Condense — the A9.6 background-coefficient clause, discharging Cycle 023's named consumer):** coefficients that set a BACKGROUND layer's growth, opacity, size, or intensity AGAINST the subject (dust-cloud growth/opacity, plate glow, reserve-field density/intensity, backdrop scale) are **subject-shaping** whenever a scoring lens can price them against the subject — a background layer's coefficients are subject-shaping when the readability or reusability lens can price them, and they must be CONFIG keys or be classified HIT; classifying them "structural" because they sit in a background generator is the named miss. Forcing evidence: T140 (Cycle 023) — the builder's census classified the dust coefficients structural while the reusability lens priced them subject-shaping at 78, the item's minimum (filed return: `hidden_files/study/recreations/T140-repetition-chant/codex/lens-reusability.md`, SCORE: 78, seat-verified at Cycle 024's Condense); INSTRUCTION Amendment A9.6 resolved the classification in the instruction layer the same cycle, and this addendum lands the same rule in the guard's own classification text. **Clarification landed 2026-11-15 (recreation Cycle 013 Condense, on T75's reusability-lens miss):** constants inside scene/asset GENERATORS that scale or shape the generated subject (skyline/scene geometry, generator seeds' geometric coefficients) are subject-shaping, not structural, whenever changing them changes what the subject looks like; font-family stacks named in CSS are likewise subject-shaping (typography is a shaping decision) and must be CONFIG keys or be classified HIT — a census that passes either class as "structural" has missed its own class. **Addendum landed 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 021 Condense, under codex-condense REVISE — invariant-exemption bar):** the declared-invariant classification is barred for any literal that implements a dimension a CONFIG key promises — if a geom.* key names it, the literals constructing it are subject-shaping and must read from the key. Forcing evidence: T130 (Cycle 021) — the car silhouette path coordinates and wheel offsets were exempted as declared invariants while geom.carW/carH promised that control; reusability lens 68.
2. **Application-site resolution.** Every CONFIG key is resolved to at least one application site in the code, **including nested and array-member keys** (`artworkTags[].level` class — Wave 6, T43). A key with no application site is a HIT (unapplied key = promised control that does not exist). **Addendum landed 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 021 Condense, under codex-condense REVISE — value-flow site validity):** an application site counts only where the key's value flows into the construction of the subject the key names (e.g. carW/carH → the car body's path/transform and wheel geometry). A key whose only occurrences are its declaration, storage, or a consumer other than its named subject (e.g. carW read only by a puff-midpoint computation) is UNAPPLIED = HIT. Forcing evidence: T130 (Cycle 021) — census HITs 0 with "geom.carW/carH → car scale declaration" while carH occurred only in its declaration and the car path/wheels were hardcoded; reusability lens 68.
3. **CSS-variable setter check.** A CONFIG key consumed through a CSS variable whose setter never assigns it is a HIT (the `geom.pieSize` false-negative class, Wave 6 T38: a reference, not a literal — invisible to literal greps, owned here explicitly).

## Output form (filed per item, pre-wave)
`config-census.md` in the item's artifact directory: literal count by class with the per-constant classification, the key→site resolution table, the CSS-variable check, and a HIT list (empty = PASS for this leg). The wave's RUN cites the path + hit count per item. The leg's verdict is ADVISORY (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling): hits are cured before scoring where the builder can, or carried into the wave printed with their disposition. The FIRING is not advisory: a wave whose record lacks the census for any item prints INCOMPLETE.

## Call point
Pre-wave, per item, after the build and BEFORE the five-lens scoring wave (wired into recreation INSTRUCTION Amendment A6 and practice INSTRUCTION Amendment A-C008). Practice re-attempts fire the same leg on the new attempt's file; the census runs against the attempt's own CONFIG, never the prior attempt's.

## Scope honesty
This leg proves the recreation is re-drivable from its CONFIG — every shaping decision named, every named key actually applied. It does not prove the CONFIG values are well chosen, the swap is beautiful, or the timing is right; the scoring wave prices those.

## Firing record
- 2026-11-15: BUILT (this file). First firing owed: the next recreation wave + the next practice wave, unconditionally.
- 2026-11-15 (recreation Cycle 013 Wave 11): FIRST FIRING ×5 items (with guard-measured-fit) — final HIT lists empty ×5 after pre-cure cures; one MISS recorded (T75: scene-generator constants + CSS font stacks passed as structural; the lens priced it) and cured by the classification clarification landed in §1 above the same Condense.
- 2026-11-16 (recreation Cycle 021 Wave 18): firing ×6 items; one MISS recorded (T130: declaration accepted as application site + car literals exempted as declared invariants; reusability lens 68 priced it) and cured the same Condense by the §1 invariant-exemption bar + §2 value-flow site-validity addenda above, re-encoded under codex-condense REVISE (the drafted site-category cure was rejected as amendment-theatre — the operative test is what counts as a site).
# guard-measured-fit — the measured-fit leg (recreation + practice)

_Built 2026-11-15 on HQ's charge (owed checks, 2026-11-15 ~08:02 EST), from the candidate spec filed in recreation Cycle-007 Wave-5's Condense and applied ad hoc since. This file is the leg's standing definition; before this build the leg existed only as candidacy text inside RUN records (Waves 5–6) and as a builder-side instrument — never as a guard with a call point. It owns ONE defect class: readability failures whose mechanism is **fit asserted, not measured** (readability stuck/under-floor in Waves 2, 3, 4, 5 and again in Wave 10 — T54 density, T57 attribution typography). It does NOT own timing anchors, density judgment, or any content-level failure; those classes stay with their own guards._

## What the leg checks (per item, on the final recreation.html)
For EVERY text element that carries subject content (titles, labels, captions-in-scene, cards, marquees, counters):
1. **A measurement row exists.** The item's build record contains a fit table with one row per subject text element: element id · container (box/strip/line) · MEASURED ink bounds after fitting (glyph ink height and width, from the build harness's geometry measurement at the 360 px stage) · container bounds · margin on the binding side. A text element with no row is a HIT.
2. **Fit is measured, never formula-claimed.** A row whose bounds are derived from the fitting formula's own assertion (font size × assumed factor) rather than from a post-fit measurement of the rendered geometry is a HIT — the formula is the claim, not the evidence (founding instances: T20's glyph-height overstatement, ink at 76% of claimed cell pitch; T19's floored 11 px phrase in a box that could not hold it; T57's attribution lines).
3. **Legibility floor.** Measured subject-text ink height below ~11 px at the 360 px stage width is a HIT, whatever the formula claimed.
4. **Hierarchy is measured too.** Where the design claims an intensity/size hierarchy between neighbouring text elements, the rows must show the measured ratio; a hue-only hierarchy against near-equal measured sizes is a HIT (T20's neighbour-window failure).

## Output form (filed per item, pre-wave)
`fit-table.md` in the item's artifact directory: the full row set + a HIT list (empty = PASS for this leg). The wave's RUN cites the table's path + hit count per item. The leg's verdict is ADVISORY (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling): hits are priced into the build before scoring where the builder can cure them; hits carried into the scoring wave are printed in the RUN with their disposition. What is NOT advisory is the firing: a wave whose record lacks the fit table for any item prints INCOMPLETE.

## Call point
Pre-wave, per item, after the build and BEFORE the five-lens scoring wave (wired into recreation INSTRUCTION Amendment A6 and practice INSTRUCTION Amendment A-C008 — the legs run before scoring, not after). Practice re-attempts fire the same leg on the new attempt's file.

## Scope honesty
This leg proves text fits its container at phone width, measured. It does not prove the text is the right text, the right density, or readable in motion — timing, density judgment, and content stay with the scoring wave and their own guards.

## Firing record
- 2026-11-15: BUILT (this file). First firing owed: the next recreation wave + the next practice wave, unconditionally.
# Guards — recreation

## The judgment layer's home (owner's order, 2026-11-14 — 100% form)
Form: **In-job form.** This job's judging = the Codex scoring wave (its review type, defined in `codex-scoring-wave.md` in this directory) + the mechanical guards below + `jev-prewave.md`. The wave's execution may be shared tooling; the definitions are this job's own files.

## The commands (mechanical guards — callable, demonstrated)
1. Construction-purity grep — recreation.html must contain no reference to source media, frames, or tracing (pattern census over the file).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (zero hits; Codex purity lens independently returned 100).
2. Swap test (parameterization proof) — headless harness loads the recreation with a swapped CONFIG phrase; target geometry must regenerate for the new words (a traced copy cannot).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1, LOGIC level (node harness, stubbed canvas: 5,060 → 2,728 targets). Pixel-level render proof is NOT exercisable from this seat (no browser binary) — printed limitation, carried in scores.md; the comparison page is the render surface.
3. Syntax + determinism check — `node --check` on inline scripts; seeded-RNG presence census.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (both scripts clean).
4. Scores arithmetic — recompute the wave average + min from the filed lens returns before scores.md prints a verdict.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (68/82/100/64 → 78.5 recomputed at filing).
5. Segment binding — segment.mp4's timestamps/duration/ffprobe facts recorded against the packet's technique window before any build starts.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (1:24–1:40 · 16.0 s · ffprobe-cited).
6. **Measured-fit leg** — `guard-measured-fit.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: every subject text element carries a MEASURED fit row (post-fit ink bounds vs container at the 360 px stage, legibility floor ~11 px); a formula-claimed fit or a missing row is a HIT. Owns the "fit asserted, not measured" readability class (Waves 2–5, 10). **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a fit table for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.
7. **Config-census leg (v3.1 codified)** — `guard-config-census.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: all-literal scan with per-constant subject-shaping/structural classification + CONFIG key→application-site resolution (nested/array keys included) + CSS-variable setter check; unapplied keys and unset-variable references are HITs. Owns the config-incompleteness reusability class. **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a census for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.

- `jev-observe-exit.md` — the Observe→Plan gate for a recreation wave (queue state priced · prior/family scores read · mechanics seen · variables extracted · flow-field model + Owner Cures 1–2 carried in working memory · consumer named). Call point: INSTRUCTION §1 gate out. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (Phase-guard augmentation, 2026-11-14.)
- `jev-plan-exit.md` — the Plan→Execute gate (parameter table · Execute + Verify checklists as instruments · failure points priced · plateau floor rule priced in Plan). Call point: INSTRUCTION §2 gate out. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (2026-11-14.)
- Deliverable Jev: **none separate, by design** — the deliverable class (a scored recreation attempt) is judged by the five-lens Codex scoring wave + §5.5 consolidation; a jev-verify file would duplicate the wave. jev-prewave is its pre-wave gate. (Printed in INSTRUCTION §5 step 3b.)
