The founding Presence print stands as the 2026-11-14 founding record; from the next cycle, the presence evaluation prices FIRING (fired / not fired — not fired = INCOMPLETE), not arming.

8. **Census-anchor probes (2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 025 Condense — mechanical layer of the allocation/count consistency landing).** Where a census anchor asserts an empty/zero state at a named instant (e.g. "no territory at 449 s"), the builder's own-checks harness MUST probe the recreation's state function at that instant (mapped segment-relative) and file the raw result in build-record.md; a prescribed schedule that is nonzero at a zero-state anchor is a build-record defect to cure BEFORE the wave, not a finding for the timing lens to discover. Forcing evidence: T160 Wave 22 (prescribed purple front 8.33% grown at the 449 s zero-state anchor; timing lens priced it 68 in `hidden_files/study/recreations/T160-expansion-front-envelopment/codex/lens-timing.md`).
# Jev call — jev-observe-exit (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Built 2026-11-14 (phase-guard augmentation). The judgment: a recreation wave may not enter Plan on a catalogue summary alone — Observe must have priced the queue, read the prior scores where they exist, seen the evidence frames, and carried the standing construction guidance into working memory._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: the wave's Observe working memory — RUN.md gate-out block (§1: mechanics summary + sub-beat timings + variable list + named consumer) for each item in the wave (up to 5 owed items).
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §1, at the gate out of Observe, before §2 Plan opens for any item in the wave. The verdict is advisory per item: applied, a RETURN-TO-OBSERVE item is held out of the wave's Plan (the wave proceeds with its advancing items; a held item never holds the wave hostage — same rule as jev-prewave §5); set aside, the miss is recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this Observe record can support a Plan for THIS recreation wave, item by item. Price, in order:
(1) QUEUE STATE PRICED — does the record state the recreation queue's current position (../QUEUE.md read this cycle), each item's queue number and OWED status, and the ownership split (Amendment A3 §3: this job owns FIRST attempts; an already-scored technique belongs to practice — a wave item that is a re-attempt is a routing defect, named)? T15 is the standing example: attempt 1 scored FAIL 78.5 on 2026-11-13, so T15 attempt 2 is practice's charge, never this job's wave item.
(2) PRIOR SCORES READ — for any item whose technique already has a scores.md or a verdict-history line (a techniques' family sibling counts: e.g. T16 shares T15's particle machinery), does the record cite the actual filed scores and cure list, or does it plan from the catalogue description alone? Planning that ignores a filed failure is the defect this leg exists to catch.
(3) MECHANICS SEEN, NOT SUMMARIZED — does the record carry the packet section's mechanics + sub-beat timings from firsthand viewing of the evidence frames and the source segment in motion (§1.1–1.2), with timings in seconds — or only a restatement of the TECHNIQUES.md one-liner? A mechanic the builder has not seen is a guess (§1.1).
(4) VARIABLES EXTRACTED — is the §1.3 variable list complete for this technique (text content, phrase count, timing/durations, palette, scale, direction, density — plus technique-specific variables), so Plan's parameter table has something to bind? A variable missing here becomes a hardcoded accident in Execute (§1.3).
(5) CONSTRUCTION GUIDANCE CARRIED — where the technique's family has standing guidance, is it IN the working memory, named? For particle-field techniques: the flow-field model (Amendment A5 §4: one constant flow field, borrowing with low-amplitude jitter, never-borrowed reserve fraction, release with velocity continuity) AND Owner Cure 1 (foreground/background intensity separation riding the borrow flag) AND Owner Cure 2 (strict phrase sequencing / phase exclusivity). For typography techniques: the typography construction precedent (Amendment A1 §2: runtime sampling of config-rendered text). Guidance that exists in INSTRUCTION but is absent from the wave's working memory is scored as absent.
(6) CONSUMER NAMED — §1.4: which production surface lifts this machinery (container type + owning lane), named per item, not "production generally."
(7) CODEX/OWNER INPUTS — were any Codex calls fired in this Observe (e.g. a record audit of a prior attempt's codex/ returns) with receipts filed, or is the absence printed? Does the wave need owner-only information (it almost never does — recreation prices from packets and filed scores); if claimed, is it routed as a named gap rather than silently assumed?
Score 0–1 per dimension (§4), classify defects from the taxonomy, return one verdict per item.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: RUN.md Observe gate-out blocks · ../QUEUE.md recreation-queue section (current) · each item's packet digest.md technique section + TECHNIQUES.md entry · prior scores.md + verdict-history.md lines for the item and its named family siblings · INSTRUCTION Amendments A1/A3/A5 guidance lines relevant to the item's family · any Observe-phase Codex receipts filed this cycle.
- NEVER FED: the builder's confidence prose · HQ's supervision faces · scores from attempts not yet filed (unfiled scores do not exist).
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; packet sections enter as the technique's section only, never the whole digest; evidence frames are referenced by path/timestamp, never fed as images (text-only judge).

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes (per item): **ADVANCE · ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE**.
- Dimensions (0–1): queue-state-priced · prior-scores-read · mechanics-seen · variables-extracted · guidance-carried · consumer-named. (1.0 = evidenced in the record with citations; 0.5 = asserted without citation; 0.0 = absent.)
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary per returned item): QUEUE-STALE (queue position/status not read this cycle) · REATTEMPT-MISROUTED (scored technique in a first-attempt wave) · SCORES-UNREAD (prior scores/cures exist, uncited) · MECHANICS-UNSEEN (no firsthand sub-beat record) · VARIABLES-THIN (variable list cannot bind a parameter table) · GUIDANCE-DROPPED (standing family guidance absent from working memory) · CONSUMER-VAGUE.
- Accepted profiles travel: cited in RUN.md at the Plan gate; ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS items carry their gap list into §2 as named Plan charges.

## 5. Floors and disposition
- Any dimension < 0.5 → RETURN-TO-OBSERVE for that item. GUIDANCE-DROPPED on a particle-field or typography item → RETURN-TO-OBSERVE at any other score (T15 attempt 1's founding lesson: the frozen-flow defect and both owner catches were guidance-shaped failures discovered only after the wave was spent).
- ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS requires every gap named with its Plan-phase cure; an unnamed gap is a RETURN.
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + primary classification + disposition land in RUN.md either way.

## Call command
- Defined prompt + context assembly per §3, executed through the fleet's sealed decision-model route named in guards.md when bound. Binding the exact runner command here is the first firing's own first step. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.**

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: (a) T15 Cycle-002's Observe record reconstructed from its RUN.md (must ADVANCE — mechanics, timings and consumer were recorded before building). (b) A constructed record proposing T15 as a wave item for attempt 2 (must RETURN-TO-OBSERVE as REATTEMPT-MISROUTED). (c) A constructed particle-technique record with no flow-field / Owner Cure 1 / Owner Cure 2 lines (must RETURN as GUIDANCE-DROPPED). (d) A constructed record whose variable list names only "text and color" for a timed multi-phrase technique (must RETURN as VARIABLES-THIN).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all four must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it. First expected amendment pressure: whether family-sibling scores (leg 2) should be mandatory for all items or only same-machinery families — priced from the first waves' returns.
# Jev call — jev-plan-exit (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Built 2026-11-14 (phase-guard augmentation). The judgment: Execute is spent only on a Plan that already contains its checklists, its failure points, and its plateau exposure — priced in Plan, never discovered at close._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: per-item Plan product in RUN.md (§2 gate out: parameter table + construction choice + verification plan) plus the wave manifest's per-item summaries.
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §2, at the gate out of Plan, before §3 Execute opens for an item. The verdict is advisory per item — applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this Plan can be executed and verified without improvisation, item by item. Price, in order:
(1) PARAMETER TABLE COMPLETE — every variable from the Observe gate-out (§1.3 list, as passed by jev-observe-exit) is a named config key with the specimen's value as default (§2.1). A variable with no key, or animation logic the Plan already describes with literals, is a Plan failure named here — not an Execute surprise.
(2) CONSTRUCTION CHOICE PRICED — the HTML-native construction is chosen for the technique's mechanics (canvas particle system · CSS keyframes · SVG draw-on · DOM animation · generated imagery + overlays) with rejected alternatives named in one line each (§2.2). For particle-field items the choice must instantiate the flow-field model (Amendment A5 §4); for typography items it must start from runtime text sampling (Amendment A1 §2) or name why not. Owner Cure 1 (intensity separation) and Owner Cure 2 (phase exclusivity) appear as construction requirements where the technique is a particle field or a sequenced-phrase piece — absent cures in a particle/sequence Plan = the T15 attempt-1 failure re-planned.
(3) EXECUTE CHECKLIST EXISTS — a step list for §3 (segment cut with timestamps ±0.5 s handles · recreation.html build · comparison.html build with synchronized controls + phone stacking · self-verify legs) concrete enough that a builder who did not write the Plan could execute it.
(4) VERIFY CHECKLIST EXISTS, AS INSTRUMENTS — §2.4's verification plan names: the swap test's exact altered values (words minimum + one timing + one palette value, §5.1) · the purity greps + asset inventory (§5.2/§6.1) · the comparison check (§5.3) · jev-prewave's call point (§5, between own checks and the wave) · the five-lens Codex wave composition (§5.4: motion fidelity · timing/tempo · construction purity · reusability · readability). A checklist item stated as an intention ("check purity") without its instrument (the grep patterns / harness) fails this leg.
(5) FAILURE POINTS EVALUATED — the Plan names what breaks it, per item: at minimum priced from this job's record — surplus-particle freeze (T15: motion 68 + reuse 64 convergent defect) · word-vs-reserve intensity collapse (owner catch 1, readability) · phrase-lifecycle overlap (owner catch 2) · fitting/wrapping limits for long words (reuse 64: fitting stops ~14 px, no multiline) · pool/density guards · the render-proof limitation (no browser binary in this seat — the comparison page is the render surface; the Plan must state how its absence is carried, as attempt 1's scores.md did). Technique-specific failure points beyond this floor are expected; a Plan whose failure list is only this floor, copied, is scored down on specificity.
(6) PLATEAU FLOOR PRICED IN PLAN — the floor rule (Amendment A2, practice §floor as close-out gate) is priced HERE: the Plan states the item's prior average where one exists in its family line or states FIRST-ATTEMPT (no prior), and names in advance what a non-beating result would mean (which lens is expected to be stuck, the hypothesized cause, the WHY the scores.md entry will carry). A re-attempt-shaped build (same machinery family, e.g. T16 on T15's pool) that reaches close-out and discovers its plateau there has failed this leg retroactively — the Condense record says so.
(7) REDUCTIONS NAMED — anything the Plan quietly drops from the packet's mechanics (a sub-beat deferred, a phase simplified) is named as a deferral with its reason (§2/jev-prewave fidelity-plan honesty); a silent deferral found here is classified, not absorbed.
Score 0–1 per dimension (§4), classify, return one verdict per item.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: RUN.md Plan gate-out blocks + wave manifest · the Observe gate-out blocks with jev-observe-exit's filed verdicts · the item's scores.md / family scores.md cure lists where they exist · INSTRUCTION §2, §5, §6 + Amendments A1/A2/A5 · guards.md command list.
- NEVER FED: builder rationale prose about expected scores · prior waves' verdicts for unrelated techniques · any lens return not yet filed.
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; recreation.html does not exist yet at this gate and is never fed.

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes (per item): **EXECUTE · REVISE · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE**.
- Dimensions (0–1): parameter-table-complete · construction-priced · execute-checklist-ready · verify-checklist-instrumented · failure-points-priced · plateau-priced.
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary): UNBOUND-VARIABLE · LITERAL-IN-LOGIC · CURE-ABSENT (Owner Cure 1/2 or family guidance missing from construction) · CHECKLIST-INTENTION (Verify item without instrument) · FAILURE-POINTS-GENERIC · PLATEAU-UNPRICED · SILENT-DEFERRAL · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE-ROOT (the Plan's defect is an Observe defect — mechanics/variables missing upstream).
- Accepted profiles travel: cited in RUN.md at the Execute gate; REVISE items carry their classified defect as the Plan amendment charge.

## 5. Floors and disposition
- verify-checklist-instrumented < 0.5 → REVISE (a Plan that cannot say how it will check itself does not execute). CURE-ABSENT on a particle-field or sequenced item → REVISE at any other score. PLATEAU-UNPRICED on a family re-use build → REVISE.
- RETURN-TO-OBSERVE is reserved for defects whose cure is upstream (missing mechanics/variables), and names the jev-observe-exit dimension that passed it — that citation is the observe-exit's firing-record evidence.
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + disposition land in RUN.md either way.

## Call command
- Defined prompt + context assembly per §3, executed through the fleet's sealed decision-model route named in guards.md when bound. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.**

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: (a) T15 attempt 1's Plan reconstructed from Cycle-002 RUN.md (must EXECUTE or REVISE only on named legs — it did carry a verification plan and a named interlude deferral). (b) A constructed particle Plan with no intensity-separation requirement (must REVISE as CURE-ABSENT). (c) A constructed Plan whose Verify checklist reads "run swap test, check purity, fire wave" with no altered values or grep patterns (must REVISE as CHECKLIST-INTENTION). (d) A constructed T16 Plan silent on T15's 78.5 and its stuck lenses (must REVISE as PLATEAU-UNPRICED).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all four must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it. Standing watch: whether the §2.4 "four lenses" wording anywhere still survives against the five-lens form — any Plan citing four lenses is a FAILURE-POINTS-GENERIC signal, not a typo.

## Amendment — allocation/count consistency leg (2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 025 Condense, pattern promotion)
**The leg:** for every item whose Plan carries a schedule or allocation table, this gate prices the table's **census-anchor mapping** before Execute: each prescribed onset/duration is mapped into segment-relative time (window-start offset applied) and cross-checked against every census state the wave record names for that item. A prescribed value that contradicts a named census state at its instant (e.g. a front prescribed 8% grown at the census instant whose state reads "no territory"; arrivals prescribed complete at 11300 ms against a census anchor "crowded by ~7000 ms") is a REVISE charge, primary class **UNBOUND-SCHEDULE**. A Plan whose schedule table prints no census-anchor mapping is priced as carrying an unmapped schedule — the mapping's absence is itself the charge (the table cannot be checked, which is the defect's hiding place). Count assertions ride the same leg: a summary count in the Plan that disagrees with the table it summarizes is UNBOUND-SCHEDULE's count form (A9.3's gate side).
**Forcing evidence (three instances, one class):** T153 Wave 21 (timing 45 — §2b allocation contradicted the §1c census checkpoints it claimed to rest on) · T160 Wave 22 (timing 68 — prescribed onsets 2500/3500 ms already 8.33%/45.45% grown at the 449 s/452 s census instants) · T162 Wave 22 (timing 64 — even-stepped allocation completing at 11300 ms against census anchors at ~2000/~7000 ms). For T160 and T162 the build implemented the prescribed schedule exactly (verified from this cycle's filed returns, bound in Cycle 025 RUN §9b item 2); for T153 the same compliance is attributed to the seat's Wave-21 cycle record (Cycle 024 RUN §5) and is unverified in the Wave-22 stream. In all three the defect was the Plan's, discovered by the timing lens at the cost of the item's verdict.
**Before/after:** before — this gate priced parameter tables for completeness and checklists for instruments, but no leg owned schedule-vs-census consistency, so three instances across Waves 21–22 shipped census-contradicting schedules through a fired gate. After — the mapping is a named Plan product and this leg prices it at the gate, where the cure costs a table edit instead of a verdict.
# Jev call — jev-prewave (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Designed 2026-11-14. The judgment: a scoring wave is the lane's expensive instrument — this call prices whether a build has EARNED its wave before the wave spends._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: an attempt's build record (parameter table · construction route · own-checks results) BEFORE the Codex wave fires.
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §4, between the builder's own checks (§5.2) and the wave (§5.3). The verdict is advisory: applied, a HOLD returns the build to the builder with named defects and the wave does not fire on that item; set aside, the miss is recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this build record shows a recreation ready to be scored, or one whose defects are already visible in its own paperwork. Price: (1) PARAMETERIZATION — is the piece genuinely CONFIG-driven (fields named, ranges sane), or hard-coded with a config costume? (2) PURITY PRE-CHECK — does the construction route describe generation from parameters, with no tracing/embedding step hiding in the prose? (3) OWN-CHECKS COMPLETENESS — are the mechanical guards' results recorded (purity grep, swap test, syntax), or asserted? (4) TECHNIQUE FIDELITY PLAN — does the record state which packet mechanics the build implements and which it deliberately defers (a deferral named is lawful; a mechanic silently absent is not)? Score 0–1 per dimension, classify defects. You judge paperwork only: a build can pass you and still fail its wave — your job is that no wave is spent on a build its own record already condemns.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: build-record.md · the CONFIG block of recreation.html · the technique's packet section · guards.md command list (what own-checks should exist) · **the item's A6 leg outputs (fit-table.md + config-census.md) — added 2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 024 Condense, on the Wave-21 pattern: prewave firings for T151, T153 and T154 all HOLDed or re-fired on feed completeness (the A6 files and packet section existed complete in the artifact directory but were not in the payload), the lane's recurring ASSERTED-CHECKS false-positive class. A payload lacking any FED element is a feeder defect, printed as such in the build record; a HOLD whose only named cure is feeding an already-filed FED element is disposed as a feed-completeness miss, with the miss recorded.**
- NEVER FED: the builder's confidence prose · prior attempts' scores (each build is priced on its own record).
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; the HTML enters as its CONFIG + construction sections, not media.

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes: **FIRE-WAVE · HOLD**.
- Dimensions (0–1): parameterization · purity pre-check · own-checks completeness · fidelity-plan honesty.
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary): HARDCODED-CONFIG · TRACING-STEP · ASSERTED-CHECKS (results claimed, not recorded) · SILENT-DEFERRAL.
- Accepted profile travels: cited in scores.md's pre-wave line; a wave fired over a HOLD is a deviation (WAVE-OVER-HOLD).

## 5. Floors and disposition
- Any dimension < 0.5 → HOLD · ASSERTED-CHECKS at any score on other dimensions → HOLD (unrecorded checks are the lane's named failure class).
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + classification + disposition land in the build record either way; an applied HOLD leaves the item IN PROGRESS and the wave moves to its next item (a held item never holds the wave hostage).

## Call command
- BINDING OWED AT FIRST FIRING — the call fires in this job's next live cycle (owner's ruling 2026-11-14: arming = firing); binding it to the fleet's decision-model runner and recording the exact command here is the first firing's own first step, and the raw response files in the run record.

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: T15 attempt 1's build record (must FIRE-WAVE: checks recorded, deferral of the interlude named in its Plan) · a constructed record claiming a swap test with no harness output (must HOLD as ASSERTED-CHECKS) · a constructed record whose CONFIG is decorative over hard-coded phrases (must HOLD as HARDCODED-CONFIG).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all three must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it.

## Owner's ruling addendum (2026-11-14 ~20:55 EST — supersedes gate phrasing above)
ARMING = FIRING: this call fires unconditionally in its owning job's next live cycle; the raw response files in the run record. There is no BUILT-NOT-FIRED state and no calibration gate — the calibration exemplars above are improvement material the firing record tests, not a precondition. VERDICTS ARE ADVISORY: applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense + the call-file amendment it teaches. Any sentence above phrasing this call as blocking, gating, or preconditioning a step reads superseded by this addendum; the FIRING is the only non-skippable part.

# Guards — recreation

## The judgment layer's home (owner's order, 2026-11-14 — 100% form)
Form: **In-job form.** This job's judging = the Codex scoring wave (its review type, defined in `codex-scoring-wave.md` in this directory) + the mechanical guards below + `jev-prewave.md`. The wave's execution may be shared tooling; the definitions are this job's own files.

## The commands (mechanical guards — callable, demonstrated)
1. Construction-purity grep — recreation.html must contain no reference to source media, frames, or tracing (pattern census over the file).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (zero hits; Codex purity lens independently returned 100).
2. Swap test (parameterization proof) — headless harness loads the recreation with a swapped CONFIG phrase; target geometry must regenerate for the new words (a traced copy cannot).
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1, LOGIC level (node harness, stubbed canvas: 5,060 → 2,728 targets). Pixel-level render proof is NOT exercisable from this seat (no browser binary) — printed limitation, carried in scores.md; the comparison page is the render surface.
3. Syntax + determinism check — `node --check` on inline scripts; seeded-RNG presence census.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (both scripts clean).
4. Scores arithmetic — recompute the wave average + min from the filed lens returns before scores.md prints a verdict.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (68/82/100/64 → 78.5 recomputed at filing).
5. Segment binding — segment.mp4's timestamps/duration/ffprobe facts recorded against the packet's technique window before any build starts.
   Demonstrated: 2026-11-13, T15 attempt 1 (1:24–1:40 · 16.0 s · ffprobe-cited).
6. **Measured-fit leg** — `guard-measured-fit.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: every subject text element carries a MEASURED fit row (post-fit ink bounds vs container at the 360 px stage, legibility floor ~11 px); a formula-claimed fit or a missing row is a HIT. Owns the "fit asserted, not measured" readability class (Waves 2–5, 10). **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a fit table for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.
7. **Config-census leg (v3.1 codified)** — `guard-config-census.md` in this directory. Per item, pre-wave: all-literal scan with per-constant subject-shaping/structural classification + CONFIG key→application-site resolution (nested/array keys included) + CSS-variable setter check; unapplied keys and unset-variable references are HITs. Owns the config-incompleteness reusability class. **Built 2026-11-15 (HQ owed-checks charge); fires unconditionally at the pre-wave step from the next cycle; a wave record lacking a census for any item prints INCOMPLETE.** Verdict advisory; firing is not.

## Jev call definitions
- `jev-prewave.md` — judges a build record's readiness to spend a scoring wave. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (Designed 2026-11-14; attempt 1 predates it and ran under the §5 panel, which runs alongside.)
- `jev-observe-exit.md` — the Observe→Plan gate for a recreation wave (queue state priced · prior/family scores read · mechanics seen · variables extracted · flow-field model + Owner Cures 1–2 carried in working memory · consumer named). Call point: INSTRUCTION §1 gate out. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (Phase-guard augmentation, 2026-11-14.)
- `jev-plan-exit.md` — the Plan→Execute gate (parameter table · Execute + Verify checklists as instruments · failure points priced · plateau floor rule priced in Plan). Call point: INSTRUCTION §2 gate out. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.** (2026-11-14.)

# Jev call — jev-prewave (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Designed 2026-11-14. The judgment: a scoring wave is the lane's expensive instrument — this call prices whether a build has EARNED its wave before the wave spends._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: an attempt's build record (parameter table · construction route · own-checks results) BEFORE the Codex wave fires.
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §4, between the builder's own checks (§5.2) and the wave (§5.3). The verdict is advisory: applied, a HOLD returns the build to the builder with named defects and the wave does not fire on that item; set aside, the miss is recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this build record shows a recreation ready to be scored, or one whose defects are already visible in its own paperwork. Price: (1) PARAMETERIZATION — is the piece genuinely CONFIG-driven (fields named, ranges sane), or hard-coded with a config costume? (2) PURITY PRE-CHECK — does the construction route describe generation from parameters, with no tracing/embedding step hiding in the prose? (3) OWN-CHECKS COMPLETENESS — are the mechanical guards' results recorded (purity grep, swap test, syntax), or asserted? (4) TECHNIQUE FIDELITY PLAN — does the record state which packet mechanics the build implements and which it deliberately defers (a deferral named is lawful; a mechanic silently absent is not)? Score 0–1 per dimension, classify defects. You judge paperwork only: a build can pass you and still fail its wave — your job is that no wave is spent on a build its own record already condemns.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: build-record.md · the CONFIG block of recreation.html · the technique's packet section · guards.md command list (what own-checks should exist) · **the item's A6 leg outputs (fit-table.md + config-census.md) — added 2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 024 Condense, on the Wave-21 pattern: prewave firings for T151, T153 and T154 all HOLDed or re-fired on feed completeness (the A6 files and packet section existed complete in the artifact directory but were not in the payload), the lane's recurring ASSERTED-CHECKS false-positive class. A payload lacking any FED element is a feeder defect, printed as such in the build record; a HOLD whose only named cure is feeding an already-filed FED element is disposed as a feed-completeness miss, with the miss recorded.**
- NEVER FED: the builder's confidence prose · prior attempts' scores (each build is priced on its own record).
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; the HTML enters as its CONFIG + construction sections, not media.

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes: **FIRE-WAVE · HOLD**.
- Dimensions (0–1): parameterization · purity pre-check · own-checks completeness · fidelity-plan honesty.
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary): HARDCODED-CONFIG · TRACING-STEP · ASSERTED-CHECKS (results claimed, not recorded) · SILENT-DEFERRAL.
- Accepted profile travels: cited in scores.md's pre-wave line; a wave fired over a HOLD is a deviation (WAVE-OVER-HOLD).

## 5. Floors and disposition
- Any dimension < 0.5 → HOLD · ASSERTED-CHECKS at any score on other dimensions → HOLD (unrecorded checks are the lane's named failure class).
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + classification + disposition land in the build record either way; an applied HOLD leaves the item IN PROGRESS and the wave moves to its next item (a held item never holds the wave hostage).

## Call command
- BINDING OWED AT FIRST FIRING — the call fires in this job's next live cycle (owner's ruling 2026-11-14: arming = firing); binding it to the fleet's decision-model runner and recording the exact command here is the first firing's own first step, and the raw response files in the run record.

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: T15 attempt 1's build record (must FIRE-WAVE: checks recorded, deferral of the interlude named in its Plan) · a constructed record claiming a swap test with no harness output (must HOLD as ASSERTED-CHECKS) · a constructed record whose CONFIG is decorative over hard-coded phrases (must HOLD as HARDCODED-CONFIG).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all three must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it.

## Owner's ruling addendum (2026-11-14 ~20:55 EST — supersedes gate phrasing above)
ARMING = FIRING: this call fires unconditionally in its owning job's next live cycle; the raw response files in the run record. There is no BUILT-NOT-FIRED state and no calibration gate — the calibration exemplars above are improvement material the firing record tests, not a precondition. VERDICTS ARE ADVISORY: applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense + the call-file amendment it teaches. Any sentence above phrasing this call as blocking, gating, or preconditioning a step reads superseded by this addendum; the FIRING is the only non-skippable part.

4. **Flow-field construction model (HQ's implementation note, standing guidance for particle techniques):** model the pool as ONE constant flow field, not per-phase animations — every particle always carries the stream's directional velocity; forming = BORROWING particles out of the flow onto targets, where they keep low-amplitude jitter (never zero velocity), with a guaranteed reserve fraction that is never borrowed; dissolving = RELEASING them back to full stream velocity with continuity (no velocity replacement at phase boundaries). The intensity separation rides the borrow flag: borrowed = foreground, reserve = background. This single model carries Owner Cure 1, Owner Cure 2's clean stage handover, and attempt 1's convergent defect (frozen surplus) at once.

## §9 — Condense
Read the run's traces + Codex returns → candidate lessons (which constructions scored well on which lenses; recurring Codex findings across techniques) → label demonstrated/candidate/planned → promote into this file (dated, technique cited) + verdict-history.md → next cycle inherits. A lens finding that recurs across two techniques is a pattern: it becomes a §6 guard candidate with the two citations.
- **Condense Codex call point:** `guards/codex-condense.md` fires at Condense close — it attacks the cycle's drafted amendments for faithfulness (does the amended text encode the lesson) and four-layer coverage (steps · mechanical guards · Jev definitions · Codex prompts; each untouched layer carries a NO-DEFECT proof or a named not-yet-amended reason). A REVISE returns the named amendment for re-encoding before the cycle record closes. Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; a cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE. (Built 2026-11-14.)

## §10 — Delivery / reporting
- Completion reported on this job's HQ.json + lane heartbeat: technique, attempt #, five lens scores + average, verdict, artifact path.
- Owner-facing presentation routes through HQ. PASS or FAIL, the report carries the scores — a FAIL with its priced gap is a complete cycle; hiding it would be the defect.

## Judgment-layer presence evaluation (standing — owner's 100% order, 2026-11-14)
Before this job's Condense closes, every cycle, evaluate the judgment layer AS HOSTED IN THIS JOB'S OWN HOME (the `guards/` directory at the home root): (a) every judge this job's deliverables demand is present as its own file there — Jev calls (instruction + call command), deterministic guards, Codex review instructions for the review types this job repeats; (b) each is current against this cycle's experience (a verdict reversed, a defect that passed, a review that missed — each either amended its file in this Condense or is named in the cycle record as not-yet-amended with its reason); (c) any judge the deliverable demanded this cycle that does not exist yet is CREATED in this step or carried as a named build charge with pilot sequencing — never noted-and-dropped. The verdict prints in the cycle record: **JUDGMENT LAYER: COMPLETE** (all demanded judges hosted + current) or **INCOMPLETE** (missing/stale judges named). INCOMPLETE is a lawful print; a silent Condense is not. JOB-05's census reads this field fleet-wide.
Founding print (2026-11-14): JUDGMENT LAYER: **INCOMPLETE** — mechanical guards hosted + demonstrated (5/5); Codex scoring wave defined in-job and demonstrated in four-lens form (five-lens form demonstrates at its first wave); jev-prewave designed, **UNBUILT** — build charge carried in this job's Condense until armed.

**Ruling addendum (2026-11-14 ~20:55 EST — owner's judgment-call ruling):** arming = firing. This job's Jev/Codex steps are non-skippable and fire unconditionally in its next live cycle, raw responses filed in the run record; verdicts are advisory — applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense + the call-file amendment it teaches. The founding print above reads superseded where it phrases any call as gated, conditional, or UNBUILT-blocked; presence from the next cycle prices whether the prescribed firings HAPPENED (a cycle closing without one is INCOMPLETE).

## Amendment A6 (2026-11-15 — HQ owed-checks charge: the two legs are BUILT and WIRED)
The measured-fit leg and the config-census leg — candidacy text since Waves 5–6 — are built as standing guards: `guards/guard-measured-fit.md` and `guards/guard-config-census.md` (v3.1 codified, including application-site resolution for nested/array keys and the CSS-variable setter check). **Pre-wave step (non-skippable):** for every item in a wave, after the build and BEFORE the five-lens scoring wave fires, both legs run on the item's final recreation.html and their outputs file in the item's artifact directory (`fit-table.md`, `config-census.md`); the wave RUN cites each leg's path + hit count per item. A wave record lacking either leg's output for any item prints **INCOMPLETE**. Leg verdicts are advisory (owner's 2026-11-14 ruling): hits are cured pre-scoring where the builder can, or carried into the wave printed with their disposition; misses (a leg's false hit or missed defect) are recorded in Condense with the guard-file amendment they teach. Scope: the legs own the fit and config classes only — timing anchors, density judgment, and content failures are NOT theirs and are never presented as cured by them.

## Amendment A7 (2026-11-15, Cycle 013 Condense — pattern rule fired: T64 + T66 timing lenses)
**Schedule basis is a required Plan field.** The §9 pattern rule fired this cycle: two techniques' timing lenses independently priced the same defect class — a recreation whose schedule is silently re-based from the segment's measured timeline (T64 lens-timing −16: calendar entrances at 0.4/1.4 s vs measured ~4/5 s · T66 lens-timing-interim1 −18: opening state mismatching the measured window). Standing requirement, binding on every Plan from Wave 12 onward: each item's window-scope line in the wave RUN.md names its **schedule basis** — `segment-anchored` (the build's phases sit at their measured segment times), `t=0 allocation` (the technique's lifecycle is re-based to the build's own zero, with the re-basing named as an allocation), or `two-act` (a measured-window act plus a labeled allocation-model act) — and the item's build-record carries the same line. A Plan whose window-scope line lacks the schedule basis is incomplete in the same way a Plan lacking its swap values is (jev-plan-exit prices it). Landed by Cycle 013's Condense under the pattern rule; HQ may amend or retire it, and until then it is operative instruction, not candidacy.

## Amendment A9 (2026-11-16, Cycle 023 Condense — the five Cycle-022 NOT-YET-AMENDED landings, plus this cycle's pattern promotion)
Landed by Cycle 023's first Replan item, the named consumer of Cycle 022 §9's five outstanding amendments. Each clause is operative instruction, binding from Wave 21 onward.
1. **Plate construction is a Plan requirement (A9.1).** Any Plan adopting a moving background plate prices its construction in the parameter table: for techniques where a moving plate IS the technique (the RSVP family form), the plate is a seamless tiled pan — repeat period = pan wrap, pan clock continuous across cycle wrap, and **tiles endpoint-periodic** (a tile whose end state differs from its start state steps at the join — T139 motion lens, Cycle 023). For a case-only stream technique (T137's form), the Plan prices a still plate — any background motion exceeds the technique.
2. **Gate-order sequencing (A9.2).** No build script is written before the Observe-exit and Plan-exit returns are filed and consumed in the wave's §2b. A draft produced earlier is a deviation printed in §2b, never silently rebuilt over.
3. **Count assertion (A9.3).** Every table-driven build prints source-count vs built-count and halts on mismatch — in the item's build-record (builder side) AND in the wave record's own summary lines (seat side): a summary count in a gate or Verify section is machine-counted against the table it summarizes before that section closes, and a mismatch blocks the section's closure until the record carries the corrected count with its source note. (Founding catches: T134's truncated sentence table, Cycle 022 · the §2b 33-vs-37 summary in Cycle 023, caught builder-side before this landing.)
4. **Raw-output filing (A9.4).** Build-records file the raw harness output (swap test, purity grep, syntax check) from the first draft, not a summary of it; a build-record whose own-checks section carries claims without the raw output beneath them is an ASSERTED-CHECKS defect at jev-prewave.
5. **Gate-out reasoning inline (A9.5).** A gate-out section carries its dispositions' reasoning inline — the construction-precedent dispositions (A1 §2's scope, A5's applicability) are stated with their reasoning in the Observe gate-out itself, not only in a later Plan section the gate judge is not fed. (Founding miss: Cycle 023's jev-observe-exit GUIDANCE-DROPPED return — the reasoning stood in §2 while the judged record was §1.)
6. **Parameterization depth (A9.6 — pattern promotion, §9 rule: the reusability finding recurred across T139 + T140 in Cycle 023, both lens-minimum at 78).** Every subject-shaping value is CONFIG data, not a code literal: vehicle/geometry branch data, background-layer geometry and growth/opacity coefficients (a background layer's coefficients are subject-shaping when a lens can price them against the subject — the T140 dust classification is resolved this way), and plate tile data. The config-census leg (A6) prices literals of these classes as HITs from Wave 21 onward; guard-config-census.md's classification text is amended in the same landing's guard sweep or carries the not-yet-amended note in the cycle record that first applies it.

## Amendment A10 (2026-11-17, Cycle 026 Condense — the three bundled restatements whose named consumer was this Condense)
Landed by Cycle 026's Condense, discharging the carry recorded in Cycle 025 §9 item 1 and Cycle 026 §0b item 1. Each clause restates at instruction level a form already operative in the guards or gate contracts; each is binding from Wave 23 onward (Wave 23 complied with all three, filed in its run record).
1. **Prewave payload completeness (A10.1 — restates §5 leg 3b at instruction level; operative since Cycle 024 in jev-prewave.md's context contract).** The jev-prewave payload for every item MUST carry that item's A6 leg outputs (fit-table.md + config-census.md) verbatim. A payload lacking either leg's output is a feeder defect printed in the wave's §5; a prewave verdict priced without them does not discharge leg 3b.
2. **Plan census-anchor mapping (A10.2 — restates the Cycle-025 Plan form at instruction level).** Every schedule table in a wave's Plan prints its census-anchor mapping: the window-start offset (segment-anchored or t=0 allocation), the census states in segment-relative ms, and the prescribed state at each instant the table governs. A schedule that departs from its census prints its departure table beside it (the Wave-22 defect class: census printed in §1, schedule derived from other numbers, the gap found only by the timing lens at verdict cost). Plan-exit judges price an absent mapping or departure table as REVISE.
3. **Motion-census Observe form (A10.3 — restates the Cycle-025 Observe form at instruction level).** §1 files a frame census at ≤2 s spacing across each item's transitions (seat-extracted, viewed firsthand by the seat), full SHA-256 per frame, with the motion findings stated per item: which instants carry motion, which are holds, and what changes between census states. Findings priced from a digest's prose alone, without the census, do not close Observe.

# Jev call — jev-observe-exit (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Built 2026-11-14 (phase-guard augmentation). The judgment: a recreation wave may not enter Plan on a catalogue summary alone — Observe must have priced the queue, read the prior scores where they exist, seen the evidence frames, and carried the standing construction guidance into working memory._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: the wave's Observe working memory — RUN.md gate-out block (§1: mechanics summary + sub-beat timings + variable list + named consumer) for each item in the wave (up to 5 owed items).
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §1, at the gate out of Observe, before §2 Plan opens for any item in the wave. The verdict is advisory per item: applied, a RETURN-TO-OBSERVE item is held out of the wave's Plan (the wave proceeds with its advancing items; a held item never holds the wave hostage — same rule as jev-prewave §5); set aside, the miss is recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this Observe record can support a Plan for THIS recreation wave, item by item. Price, in order:
(1) QUEUE STATE PRICED — does the record state the recreation queue's current position (../QUEUE.md read this cycle), each item's queue number and OWED status, and the ownership split (Amendment A3 §3: this job owns FIRST attempts; an already-scored technique belongs to practice — a wave item that is a re-attempt is a routing defect, named)? T15 is the standing example: attempt 1 scored FAIL 78.5 on 2026-11-13, so T15 attempt 2 is practice's charge, never this job's wave item.
(2) PRIOR SCORES READ — for any item whose technique already has a scores.md or a verdict-history line (a techniques' family sibling counts: e.g. T16 shares T15's particle machinery), does the record cite the actual filed scores and cure list, or does it plan from the catalogue description alone? Planning that ignores a filed failure is the defect this leg exists to catch.
(3) MECHANICS SEEN, NOT SUMMARIZED — does the record carry the packet section's mechanics + sub-beat timings from firsthand viewing of the evidence frames and the source segment in motion (§1.1–1.2), with timings in seconds — or only a restatement of the TECHNIQUES.md one-liner? A mechanic the builder has not seen is a guess (§1.1).
(4) VARIABLES EXTRACTED — is the §1.3 variable list complete for this technique (text content, phrase count, timing/durations, palette, scale, direction, density — plus technique-specific variables), so Plan's parameter table has something to bind? A variable missing here becomes a hardcoded accident in Execute (§1.3).
(5) CONSTRUCTION GUIDANCE CARRIED — where the technique's family has standing guidance, is it IN the working memory, named? For particle-field techniques: the flow-field model (Amendment A5 §4: one constant flow field, borrowing with low-amplitude jitter, never-borrowed reserve fraction, release with velocity continuity) AND Owner Cure 1 (foreground/background intensity separation riding the borrow flag) AND Owner Cure 2 (strict phrase sequencing / phase exclusivity). For typography techniques: the typography construction precedent (Amendment A1 §2: runtime sampling of config-rendered text). Guidance that exists in INSTRUCTION but is absent from the wave's working memory is scored as absent.
(6) CONSUMER NAMED — §1.4: which production surface lifts this machinery (container type + owning lane), named per item, not "production generally."
(7) CODEX/OWNER INPUTS — were any Codex calls fired in this Observe (e.g. a record audit of a prior attempt's codex/ returns) with receipts filed, or is the absence printed? Does the wave need owner-only information (it almost never does — recreation prices from packets and filed scores); if claimed, is it routed as a named gap rather than silently assumed?
Score 0–1 per dimension (§4), classify defects from the taxonomy, return one verdict per item.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: RUN.md Observe gate-out blocks · ../QUEUE.md recreation-queue section (current) · each item's packet digest.md technique section + TECHNIQUES.md entry · prior scores.md + verdict-history.md lines for the item and its named family siblings · INSTRUCTION Amendments A1/A3/A5 guidance lines relevant to the item's family · any Observe-phase Codex receipts filed this cycle.
- NEVER FED: the builder's confidence prose · HQ's supervision faces · scores from attempts not yet filed (unfiled scores do not exist).
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; packet sections enter as the technique's section only, never the whole digest; evidence frames are referenced by path/timestamp, never fed as images (text-only judge).

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes (per item): **ADVANCE · ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE**.
- Dimensions (0–1): queue-state-priced · prior-scores-read · mechanics-seen · variables-extracted · guidance-carried · consumer-named. (1.0 = evidenced in the record with citations; 0.5 = asserted without citation; 0.0 = absent.)
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary per returned item): QUEUE-STALE (queue position/status not read this cycle) · REATTEMPT-MISROUTED (scored technique in a first-attempt wave) · SCORES-UNREAD (prior scores/cures exist, uncited) · MECHANICS-UNSEEN (no firsthand sub-beat record) · VARIABLES-THIN (variable list cannot bind a parameter table) · GUIDANCE-DROPPED (standing family guidance absent from working memory) · CONSUMER-VAGUE.
- Accepted profiles travel: cited in RUN.md at the Plan gate; ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS items carry their gap list into §2 as named Plan charges.

## 5. Floors and disposition
- Any dimension < 0.5 → RETURN-TO-OBSERVE for that item. GUIDANCE-DROPPED on a particle-field or typography item → RETURN-TO-OBSERVE at any other score (T15 attempt 1's founding lesson: the frozen-flow defect and both owner catches were guidance-shaped failures discovered only after the wave was spent).
- ADVANCE-WITH-GAPS requires every gap named with its Plan-phase cure; an unnamed gap is a RETURN.
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + primary classification + disposition land in RUN.md either way.

## Call command
- Defined prompt + context assembly per §3, executed through the fleet's sealed decision-model route named in guards.md when bound. Binding the exact runner command here is the first firing's own first step. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.**

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: (a) T15 Cycle-002's Observe record reconstructed from its RUN.md (must ADVANCE — mechanics, timings and consumer were recorded before building). (b) A constructed record proposing T15 as a wave item for attempt 2 (must RETURN-TO-OBSERVE as REATTEMPT-MISROUTED). (c) A constructed particle-technique record with no flow-field / Owner Cure 1 / Owner Cure 2 lines (must RETURN as GUIDANCE-DROPPED). (d) A constructed record whose variable list names only "text and color" for a timed multi-phrase technique (must RETURN as VARIABLES-THIN).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all four must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it. First expected amendment pressure: whether family-sibling scores (leg 2) should be mandatory for all items or only same-machinery families — priced from the first waves' returns.
# Jev call — jev-plan-exit (recreation)

_Per-call definition from jev-call-template.md. Built 2026-11-14 (phase-guard augmentation). The judgment: Execute is spent only on a Plan that already contains its checklists, its failure points, and its plateau exposure — priced in Plan, never discovered at close._

## 1. What it judges
- Deliverable: per-item Plan product in RUN.md (§2 gate out: parameter table + construction choice + verification plan) plus the wave manifest's per-item summaries.
- Call point: recreation INSTRUCTION §2, at the gate out of Plan, before §3 Execute opens for an item. The verdict is advisory per item — applied, or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. An unfired call makes the cycle INCOMPLETE.

## 2. Instruction (the model's charge)
Judge whether this Plan can be executed and verified without improvisation, item by item. Price, in order:
(1) PARAMETER TABLE COMPLETE — every variable from the Observe gate-out (§1.3 list, as passed by jev-observe-exit) is a named config key with the specimen's value as default (§2.1). A variable with no key, or animation logic the Plan already describes with literals, is a Plan failure named here — not an Execute surprise.
(2) CONSTRUCTION CHOICE PRICED — the HTML-native construction is chosen for the technique's mechanics (canvas particle system · CSS keyframes · SVG draw-on · DOM animation · generated imagery + overlays) with rejected alternatives named in one line each (§2.2). For particle-field items the choice must instantiate the flow-field model (Amendment A5 §4); for typography items it must start from runtime text sampling (Amendment A1 §2) or name why not. Owner Cure 1 (intensity separation) and Owner Cure 2 (phase exclusivity) appear as construction requirements where the technique is a particle field or a sequenced-phrase piece — absent cures in a particle/sequence Plan = the T15 attempt-1 failure re-planned.
(3) EXECUTE CHECKLIST EXISTS — a step list for §3 (segment cut with timestamps ±0.5 s handles · recreation.html build · comparison.html build with synchronized controls + phone stacking · self-verify legs) concrete enough that a builder who did not write the Plan could execute it.
(4) VERIFY CHECKLIST EXISTS, AS INSTRUMENTS — §2.4's verification plan names: the swap test's exact altered values (words minimum + one timing + one palette value, §5.1) · the purity greps + asset inventory (§5.2/§6.1) · the comparison check (§5.3) · jev-prewave's call point (§5, between own checks and the wave) · the five-lens Codex wave composition (§5.4: motion fidelity · timing/tempo · construction purity · reusability · readability). A checklist item stated as an intention ("check purity") without its instrument (the grep patterns / harness) fails this leg.
(5) FAILURE POINTS EVALUATED — the Plan names what breaks it, per item: at minimum priced from this job's record — surplus-particle freeze (T15: motion 68 + reuse 64 convergent defect) · word-vs-reserve intensity collapse (owner catch 1, readability) · phrase-lifecycle overlap (owner catch 2) · fitting/wrapping limits for long words (reuse 64: fitting stops ~14 px, no multiline) · pool/density guards · the render-proof limitation (no browser binary in this seat — the comparison page is the render surface; the Plan must state how its absence is carried, as attempt 1's scores.md did). Technique-specific failure points beyond this floor are expected; a Plan whose failure list is only this floor, copied, is scored down on specificity.
(6) PLATEAU FLOOR PRICED IN PLAN — the floor rule (Amendment A2, practice §floor as close-out gate) is priced HERE: the Plan states the item's prior average where one exists in its family line or states FIRST-ATTEMPT (no prior), and names in advance what a non-beating result would mean (which lens is expected to be stuck, the hypothesized cause, the WHY the scores.md entry will carry). A re-attempt-shaped build (same machinery family, e.g. T16 on T15's pool) that reaches close-out and discovers its plateau there has failed this leg retroactively — the Condense record says so.
(7) REDUCTIONS NAMED — anything the Plan quietly drops from the packet's mechanics (a sub-beat deferred, a phase simplified) is named as a deferral with its reason (§2/jev-prewave fidelity-plan honesty); a silent deferral found here is classified, not absorbed.
Score 0–1 per dimension (§4), classify, return one verdict per item.

## 3. Context contract (masked view)
- FED: RUN.md Plan gate-out blocks + wave manifest · the Observe gate-out blocks with jev-observe-exit's filed verdicts · the item's scores.md / family scores.md cure lists where they exist · INSTRUCTION §2, §5, §6 + Amendments A1/A2/A5 · guards.md command list.
- NEVER FED: builder rationale prose about expected scores · prior waves' verdicts for unrelated techniques · any lens return not yet filed.
- Ceiling: ≤32k tokens; recreation.html does not exist yet at this gate and is never fed.

## 4. Output space
- Verdict classes (per item): **EXECUTE · REVISE · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE**.
- Dimensions (0–1): parameter-table-complete · construction-priced · execute-checklist-ready · verify-checklist-instrumented · failure-points-priced · plateau-priced.
- Rejection taxonomy (one primary): UNBOUND-VARIABLE · LITERAL-IN-LOGIC · CURE-ABSENT (Owner Cure 1/2 or family guidance missing from construction) · CHECKLIST-INTENTION (Verify item without instrument) · FAILURE-POINTS-GENERIC · PLATEAU-UNPRICED · SILENT-DEFERRAL · RETURN-TO-OBSERVE-ROOT (the Plan's defect is an Observe defect — mechanics/variables missing upstream).
- Accepted profiles travel: cited in RUN.md at the Execute gate; REVISE items carry their classified defect as the Plan amendment charge.

## 5. Floors and disposition
- verify-checklist-instrumented < 0.5 → REVISE (a Plan that cannot say how it will check itself does not execute). CURE-ABSENT on a particle-field or sequenced item → REVISE at any other score. PLATEAU-UNPRICED on a family re-use build → REVISE.
- RETURN-TO-OBSERVE is reserved for defects whose cure is upstream (missing mechanics/variables), and names the jev-observe-exit dimension that passed it — that citation is the observe-exit's firing-record evidence.
- Disposition: the verdict is advisory — reviewed by the runner, applied or set aside with the miss recorded in Condense. The verdict + disposition land in RUN.md either way.

## Call command
- Defined prompt + context assembly per §3, executed through the fleet's sealed decision-model route named in guards.md when bound. **Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle. A cycle in which it did not fire prints INCOMPLETE.**

## 6. Firing record and improvement
- Exemplars: (a) T15 attempt 1's Plan reconstructed from Cycle-002 RUN.md (must EXECUTE or REVISE only on named legs — it did carry a verification plan and a named interlude deferral). (b) A constructed particle Plan with no intensity-separation requirement (must REVISE as CURE-ABSENT). (c) A constructed Plan whose Verify checklist reads "run swap test, check purity, fire wave" with no altered values or grep patterns (must REVISE as CHECKLIST-INTENTION). (d) A constructed T16 Plan silent on T15's 78.5 and its stuck lenses (must REVISE as PLATEAU-UNPRICED).
- Firing: this call fires at its step on every cycle; its firing record and misses are its improvement material. No test precedes firing. The exemplars above stand as its first firing cases — all four must classify correctly when fired, and a miss is recorded in Condense as improvement material, never as a bar to firing.

## 7. Iteration (Condense)
Amendments name forcing evidence + before/after. A text change takes effect on the call's next firing; the call keeps firing every cycle, and Condense prices the change against the returns that follow it. Standing watch: whether the §2.4 "four lenses" wording anywhere still survives against the five-lens form — any Plan citing four lenses is a FAILURE-POINTS-GENERIC signal, not a typo.

## Amendment — allocation/count consistency leg (2026-11-16, recreation Cycle 025 Condense, pattern promotion)
**The leg:** for every item whose Plan carries a schedule or allocation table, this gate prices the table's **census-anchor mapping** before Execute: each prescribed onset/duration is mapped into segment-relative time (window-start offset applied) and cross-checked against every census state the wave record names for that item. A prescribed value that contradicts a named census state at its instant (e.g. a front prescribed 8% grown at the census instant whose state reads "no territory"; arrivals prescribed complete at 11300 ms against a census anchor "crowded by ~7000 ms") is a REVISE charge, primary class **UNBOUND-SCHEDULE**. A Plan whose schedule table prints no census-anchor mapping is priced as carrying an unmapped schedule — the mapping's absence is itself the charge (the table cannot be checked, which is the defect's hiding place). Count assertions ride the same leg: a summary count in the Plan that disagrees with the table it summarizes is UNBOUND-SCHEDULE's count form (A9.3's gate side).
**Forcing evidence (three instances, one class):** T153 Wave 21 (timing 45 — §2b allocation contradicted the §1c census checkpoints it claimed to rest on) · T160 Wave 22 (timing 68 — prescribed onsets 2500/3500 ms already 8.33%/45.45% grown at the 449 s/452 s census instants) · T162 Wave 22 (timing 64 — even-stepped allocation completing at 11300 ms against census anchors at ~2000/~7000 ms). For T160 and T162 the build implemented the prescribed schedule exactly (verified from this cycle's filed returns, bound in Cycle 025 RUN §9b item 2); for T153 the same compliance is attributed to the seat's Wave-21 cycle record (Cycle 024 RUN §5) and is unverified in the Wave-22 stream. In all three the defect was the Plan's, discovered by the timing lens at the cost of the item's verdict.
**Before/after:** before — this gate priced parameter tables for completeness and checklists for instruments, but no leg owned schedule-vs-census consistency, so three instances across Waves 21–22 shipped census-contradicting schedules through a fired gate. After — the mapping is a named Plan product and this leg prices it at the gate, where the cure costs a table edit instead of a verdict.
